TL;DR: 本文提出BronchoLumen，一种基于YOLO的实时支气管镜孔口检测系统。比较YOLOv8和YOLOv12在公开数据集上的表现，YOLOv8在mAP@0.5上略优（0.91 vs 0.84），YOLOv12定位精度稍好（mAP@0.5:0.9为0.48 vs 0.45），系统在多数场景下表现出鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 支气管镜检查中导航复杂气道分支困难，现有导航辅助系统缺乏实时、准确的支气管孔口检测方法，且缺乏跨图像域鲁棒性验证。

Method: 基于YOLOv8和YOLOv12两种目标检测架构，在公开数据集上训练和测试，比较mAP@0.5和mAP@0.5:0.9两个指标，实现实时支气管孔口检测系统BronchoLumen。

Result: YOLOv8在域内测试集mAP@0.5为0.91，跨域测试集为0.68；YOLOv12分别为0.84和0.68，定位精度略高（0.48/0.26 vs 0.45/0.25）。系统对运动模糊和低对比度存在一定不确定性，但整体鲁棒。

Conclusion: BronchoLumen作为开源系统，在不同图像域中实现了高精度和高效率的支气管孔口检测。YOLOv12定位更准但精度略低于YOLOv8，模型已公开发布以促进后续研究。

Abstract: Bronchoscopy is routinely conducted in pulmonary clinics and intensive care units, but navigating the complex branching of the respiratory tract remains challenging. This paper introduces BronchoLumen, a real-time YOLO-based system for detecting bronchial orifices in video bronchoscopy, aiming to assist navigation and CAD systems. The paper investigates if bronchial orifices can be robustly detected across image domains using state-of-the-art object detection and a limited set of public image data. The study includes the description and comparison of YOLOv8, a widely adopted architecture, and YOLOv12, a more recent architecture integrating attention-based modules to improve spatial reasoning. Both models are trained and tested solely on publicly available datasets comprising different image domains. A comparison of both models is conducted based on the common metrics mAP@0.5 and mAP@0.5:0.9 with the latter emphasizing localization accuracy. For YOLOv8 we obtained a mAP@0.5 of 0.91 on an in-domain and 0.68 on a cross-domain test set. YOLOv12 achieved 0.84 and 0.68 respectively with slightly better localization accuracy with mAP@0.5:0.9 of 0.48 and 0.26 compared to YOLOv8 with 0.45 and 0.25. Challenges like motion blur and low contrast occasionally entailed uncertainties but the system demonstrated overall robustness in most scenarios. BronchoLumen is an open-weight, YOLO-based solution for bronchial orifice detection offering high accuracy and efficiency across multiple image domains. While the more recent YOLOv12 achieves better localization accuracy, we observed a slightly worse precision. The models have been made publicly available to foster further research in bronchoscopy navigation.

</details>


### [74] [Focusable Monocular Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11756)
*Yuxin Du,Tao Lin,Zile Zhong,Runting Li,Xiyao Chen,Jiting Liu,Chenglin Liu,Ying-Cong Chen,Yuqian Fu,Bo Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出聚焦式单目深度估计任务(FDE)及FocusDepth框架，通过区域感知的深度估计方法，利用SAM3和Depth Anything的多尺度空间对齐融合，在保持全局几何结构的同时优先提升目标区域的深度精度和边界清晰度。在构建的FDE-Bench基准上，该方法显著优于全局微调基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的单目深度基础模型采用统一的逐像素损失优化，无法区分用户指定的目标区域与周围上下文，导致在任务关键区域（如目标前景和边界）的深度精度不足。

Method: 提出FocusDepth框架：核心是Multi-Scale Spatial-Aligned Fusion (MSSA)模块，该模块将Segment Anything Model 3的多尺度特征进行空间对齐后注入Depth Anything系列模型，通过尺度特定的门控条件融合实现密集提示注入，同时不破坏几何表征。

Result: 在构建的FDE-Bench基准（包含25.29万/7.25万训练/验证三元组，972个类别）上，FocusDepth在框提示和文本提示下均一致优于全局微调的DA2/DA3基线，尤其在目标边界和前景区域提升最大；消融实验表明空间对齐是关键，破坏提示-几何对应可使AbsRel增加高达13.8%。

Conclusion: 通过区域感知的深度估计范式，FocusDepth有效实现了对指定目标区域的深度精度优先化，同时保持全局场景几何一致性，为下游任务提供可聚焦的深度感知能力。

Abstract: Monocular depth foundation models generalize well across scenes, yet they are typically optimized with uniform pixel-wise objectives that do not distinguish user-specified or task-relevant target regions from the surrounding context. We therefore introduce Focusable Monocular Depth Estimation (FDE), a region-aware depth estimation task in which, given a specified target region, the model is required to prioritize foreground depth accuracy, preserve sharp boundary transitions, and maintain coherent global scene geometry. To prioritize task-critical region modeling, we propose FocusDepth, a prompt-conditioned monocular relative depth estimation framework that guides depth modeling to focus on target regions via box/text prompts. The core Multi-Scale Spatial-Aligned Fusion (MSSA) in FocusDepth spatially aligns multi-scale features from Segment Anything Model 3 to the Depth Anything family and injects them through scale-specific, gated conditional fusion. This enables dense prompt cue injection without disrupting geometric representations, thereby endowing the depth estimation model with focused perception capability. To study FDE, we establish FDE-Bench, a target-centric monocular relative depth benchmark built from image-target-depth triplets across five datasets, containing 252.9K/72.5K train/val triplets and 972 categories spanning real-world and embodied simulation environments. On FDE-Bench, FocusDepth consistently improves over globally fine-tuned DA2/DA3 baselines under both box and text prompts, with the largest gains appearing in target boundary and foreground regions while preserving global scene geometry. Ablations show that MSSA's spatial alignment is the key design factor, as disrupting prompt-geometry correspondence increases AbsRel by up to 13.8%.

</details>


### [75] [M$^4$-SAM: Multi-Modal Mixture-of-Experts with Memory-Augmented SAM for RGB-D Video Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11760)
*Jiyuan Liu,Jia Lin,Xiaofei Zhou,Runmin Cong,Deyang Liu,Zhi Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对SAM2在RGB-D视频显著性目标检测中的三大问题（线性LoRA空间建模有限、多尺度特征利用不足、依赖显式提示初始化），提出M⁴-SAM方法，通过模态感知MoE-LoRA、门控多级特征融合和伪引导初始化，在三个公开数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAM2作为通用分割基础模型在RGB-D视频显著性目标检测中面临三个挑战：线性LoRA空间建模能力有限、未能充分利用SAM的多尺度特征、需要显式提示进行初始化。

Method: 1) 模态感知MoE-LoRA：在SAM2编码器中注入卷积专家以编码局部空间先验，并引入模态调度器实现高效多模态微调；2) 门控多级特征融合：通过自适应门控机制分层聚合多尺度编码器特征，平衡空间细节与语义上下文；3) 伪引导初始化：将粗分割掩码作为伪先验来引导记忆库初始化，实现无需人工提示的零样本VSOD。

Result: 在三个公开RGB-D VSOD数据集上，所有评估指标均达到最先进水平。

Conclusion: M⁴-SAM通过多模态专家混合与记忆增强策略，有效解决了SAM2在RGB-D VSOD中的三大局限，验证了参数高效微调与层次化特征融合在该任务中的有效性。

Abstract: The Segment Anything Model 2 (SAM2) has emerged as a foundation model for universal segmentation. Owing to its generalizable visual representations, SAM2 has been successfully applied to various downstream tasks. However, extending SAM2 to the RGB-D video salient object detection (RGB-D VSOD) task encounters three challenges including limited spatial modeling of linear LoRA, insufficient employment of SAM's multi-scale features, and dependence of initialization on explicit prompts. To address the issues, we present Multi-Modal Mixture-of-Experts with Memory-Augmented SAM (M$^4$-SAM), which equips SAM2 with modality-related PEFT, hierarchical feature fusion, and prompt-free memory initialization. Firstly, we inject Modality-Aware MoE-LORA, which employs convolutional experts to encode local spatial priors and introduces a modality dispatcher for efficient multi-modal fine-tuning, into SAM2's encoder. Secondly, we deploy Gated Multi-Level Feature Fusion, which hierarchically aggregates multi-scale encoder features with an adaptive gating mechanism, to balance spatial details and semantic context. Finally, to conduct zero-shot VSOD without manual prompts, we utilize a Pseudo-Guided Initialization, where a coarse mask is regarded as a pseudo prior and used to bootstrap the memory bank. Extensive experiments demonstrate that M$^4$-SAM achieves the state-of-the-art performance across all evaluation metrics on three public RGB-D VSOD datasets.

</details>


### [76] [Revisiting Shadow Detection from a Vision-Language Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11771)
*Yonghui Wang,Wengang Zhou,Hao Feng,Houqiang Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SVL框架，通过引入语言作为显式语义参考来解决视觉模糊条件下的阴影检测问题，仅用不到1%的可训练参数即可实现强鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统阴影检测方法依赖像素级视觉监督，在视觉模糊场景（如深色表面与阴影混淆）中难以建立稳定决策规则。

Method: 提出SVL（Shadow Vision-Language）框架，包含：1）场景级阴影比率回归实现图像-文本对齐；2）全局到局部的耦合机制；3）局部patch级文本约束。基于冻结的DINOv3编码器，仅训练轻量投影和解码模块。

Result: 在多个阴影检测基准上取得强性能，尤其在视觉模糊条件下的鲁棒性显著提升。

Conclusion: 语言作为显式语义参考能有效辅助阴影检测，视觉-语言融合是解决视觉模糊歧义的有前途方向。

Abstract: Shadow detection is commonly formulated as a vision-driven dense prediction problem, where models rely primarily on pixel-wise visual supervision to distinguish shadows from non-shadow regions. However, this formulation can become unreliable in visually ambiguous cases, where similar dark regions may correspond either to cast shadows or to intrinsically dark surfaces, making visual evidence alone insufficient for establishing a stable decision rule. In this work, we revisit shadow detection from a vision--language perspective and argue that robust prediction benefits from an explicit semantic reference beyond visual cues alone. We propose SVL, a Shadow Vision--Language framework that uses language as an explicit semantic reference to disambiguate shadows from visually similar dark regions. SVL aligns the global image representation with shadow-related text embeddings through a scene-level shadow ratio regression objective, thereby providing image-level guidance on the overall extent of shadows. To transfer this global guidance to dense inference, SVL introduces a global-to-local coupling mechanism that enforces consistency between image-level guidance and patch-level predictions. In parallel, SVL applies local patch-level constraints with text embeddings to improve fine-grained discrimination under challenging appearance conditions. Built on a frozen DINOv3 image encoder, the framework learns only lightweight projection and decoding modules, yielding a parameter-efficient design with less than $1\%$ trainable parameters. Extensive experiments on multiple shadow detection benchmarks, including dedicated hard-case evaluations, suggest strong overall performance and improved robustness under visually ambiguous conditions.

</details>


### [77] [Urban Risk-Aware Navigation via VQA-Based Event Maps for People with Low Vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11782)
*Antoni Valls,Jordi Sanchez-Riera*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了基于视觉问答的事件地图框架，利用视觉语言模型为视障人士实现跨场景的行人场景描述与危险识别，采用三级层次化查询结构和加权风险评分系统生成可导航的风险感知事件地图。在跨六大洲20个城市的基准测试中，生成式多模态大语言模型（特别是Qwen-VL）显著优于传统分类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 全球数亿视障人士在城市环境中的安全导航受到严重限制，现有可穿戴辅助设备依赖特定任务的视觉流水线，缺乏灵活性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出基于视觉问答的事件地图框架，利用VLM进行场景描述与危险识别；设计三级层次化查询结构实现细粒度场景理解；聚合模型响应至加权风险评分系统，将街道段映射为四个离散安全类别，生成风险感知事件地图。

Result: 构建了覆盖六大洲20个城市的多样化数据集（800+图像，18,000+问答）；基准测试四种VQA架构，生成式MLLMs（Qwen-VL）在精度与召回率平衡上表现最佳。

Conclusion: 结果表明MLLMs可作为视障人士辅助导航系统灵活且可泛化的基础方案，验证了其替代传统任务特定视觉流水线的可行性。

Abstract: Visual impairment affects hundreds of millions of people worldwide, severely limiting their ability to navigate urban environments safely and independently. While wearable assistive devices offer a promising platform for real-time hazard detection, existing approaches rely on task-specific vision pipelines that lack flexibility and generalizability. In this work, we propose an event map framework based on visual question answering that leverages Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for pedestrian scene description and hazard identification across diverse real-world environments, using a three-level hierarchical query structure to enable fine-grained scene understanding without task-specific retraining. Model responses are aggregated into a weighted risk scoring system that maps street segments into four discrete safety categories, producing navigable risk-aware event maps for route planning. To support evaluation and future research, we introduce a geographically diverse dataset spanning 20 cities across six continents, comprising over 800 annotated images and 18,000 answered questions. We benchmark four VQA architectures -ViLT, LLaVA, InstructBLIP, and Qwen-VL- and find that generative Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) substantially outperform classification-based approaches, with Qwen-VL achieving the best overall balance of precision and recall. These results demonstrate the viability of MLLMs as a flexible and generalizable foundation for assistive navigation systems for visually impaired people.

</details>


### [78] [SB-BEVFusion: Enhancing the Robustness against Sensor Malfunction and Corruptions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11799)
*Markus Essl,Marta Moscati,Mubashir Noman,Muhammad Zaigham Zaheer,Usman Naseem,Shah Nawaz,Markus Schedl*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种框架无关的多模态融合模块，能够在摄像头或LiDAR数据缺失或损坏时仍保持良好性能，在MultiCorrupt数据集上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有BEV融合方法在理想条件下有效，但当某一传感器数据缺失、损坏或受噪声干扰时性能严重下降，缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 设计了一个框架无关的融合模块，可应对任一模态缺失或损坏的情况，并在BEVFusion框架中实例化验证。

Result: 在MultiCorrupt数据集上，该方法在传感器缺失/损坏场景下显著优于现有统一表示方法，在极端天气和传感器故障场景下达到SOTA。

Conclusion: 所提模块有效提升了多模态3D目标检测在传感器故障或恶劣条件下的鲁棒性，且具有框架无关性。

Abstract: Multimodal sensor fusion has demonstrated remarkable performance improvements over unimodal approaches in 3D object detection for autonomous vehicles. Typically, existing methods transform multimodal data from independent sensors, such as camera and LiDAR, into a unified bird's-eye view (BEV) representation for fusion. Although effective in ideal conditions, this strategy suffers from substantial performance deterioration when camera or LiDAR data are missing, corrupted, or noisy. To address this vulnerability, we develop a framework-agnostic fusion module for camera and LiDAR data that allows for handling cases when one of the two modalities is missing or corrupted. To demonstrate the effectiveness of our module, we instantiate it in BEVFusion [1], a well-established framework to combine camera and LiDAR data for 3D object detection. By means of quantitative experiments on the MultiCorrupt dataset, we demonstrate that our module achieves favorable performance improvements under scenarios of missing and corrupted modalities, substantially outperforming existing unified representation approaches across a wide range of sensor deterioration scenarios and reaching state-of-the-art performance in scenarios of corrupted modality due to extreme weather conditions and sensor failure.

</details>


### [79] [OTT-Vid: Optimal Transport Temporal Token Compression for Video Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11803)
*Minseok Kang,Minhyeok Lee,Jungho Lee,Minjung Kim,Donghyeong Kim,Dayeon Lee,Heeseung Choi,Ig-jae Kim,Sangyoun Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OTT-Vid，一种基于最优传输（OT）的视频令牌压缩框架，通过空间剪枝保留帧内代表性令牌，并利用非均匀质量与位置感知代价的OT在相邻帧间估计时间可压缩性，动态分配压缩预算。在仅保留10%令牌的情况下，在视频问答和时序定位基准上达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Video-LLMs在处理长视频时因视觉令牌积压导致推理成本快速增长，现有无训练令牌压缩方法仅依赖跨帧相似度或分割启发式，忽略令牌在帧内的语义角色，且无法自适应调整压缩强度。

Method: 两阶段框架：1）空间剪枝——识别每帧内代表性内容；2）最优传输——在相邻帧间求解OT，采用非均匀令牌质量保护重要令牌，位置感知代价结合特征与空间差异，用传输难度动态分配压缩预算。

Result: 在六个基准上，仅保留10%令牌即保持95.8%的VQA性能和73.9%的VTG性能，一致优于现有无训练压缩方法。

Conclusion: 基于最优传输的令牌压缩框架能有效权衡令牌重要性与匹配代价，实现自适应、语义感知的高效视频令牌压缩。

Abstract: As Video Large Language Models (Video-LLMs) scale to longer and more complex videos, their inference cost grows rapidly due to the large volume of visual tokens accumulated across frames. Training-free token compression has emerged as a practical solution to this bottleneck. However, existing temporal compression methods rely primarily on cross-frame token similarity or segmentation heuristics, overlooking each token's semantic role within its frame and failing to adapt compression strength to the compressibility of each frame pair. In this work, we propose OTT-Vid, a transport-derived allocation framework for temporal token compression. Our approach consists of two stages: spatial pruning identifies representative content within each frame, and optimal transport (OT) is then solved between neighboring frames to estimate temporal compressibility. We formulate this OT with non-uniform token mass, which protects semantically important tokens from aggressive compression, and a locality-aware cost that captures both feature and spatial disparities. The resulting transport plan jointly balances token importance and matching cost, while its total cost defines the transport difficulty of each frame pair, which we use to allocate compression budgets dynamically. Experiments on six benchmarks spanning video question answering and temporal grounding show that OTT-Vid preserves 95.8% of VQA and 73.9% of VTG performance while retaining only 10% of tokens, consistently outperforming existing state-of-the-art training-free compression methods.

</details>


### [80] [Mitigating Action-Relation Hallucinations in LVLMs via Relation-aware Visual Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11808)
*Zhenxin Qin,Qiang Li,Qingzhuo Wang,Ruiyang Qin,Zhihua Wei,Wen Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种通过定位动作相关图像区域并增强LVLM对其注意力来减轻动作关系幻觉的方法（ARS评分+RVE增强），在几乎不增加推理成本的情况下显著优于现有基线，并能泛化到空间关系和物体幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LVLM幻觉研究主要集中在物体幻觉上，忽视了更复杂的动作关系幻觉；作者实证发现动作关系幻觉的主要原因是LVLM对视觉信息的注意力不足。

Method: 1) 定义Action-Relation Sensitivity (ARS)评分，识别对动作关系变化最敏感的注意力头，定位包含关键视觉线索的动作相关图像区域；2) 提出Relation-aware Visual Enhancement (RVE)方法，增强LVLM对这些区域的注意力。

Result: 与现有基线相比，该方法在减轻动作关系幻觉方面取得了优越性能，且仅增加可忽略的推理成本；同时能有效泛化到空间关系幻觉和物体幻觉。

Conclusion: 通过增强LVLM对动作相关视觉区域的注意力，可以有效缓解多种类型的幻觉（动作关系、空间关系、物体），且推理成本极低，具有良好泛化能力。

Abstract: Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable performance on diverse vision-language tasks. However, LVLMs still suffer from hallucinations, generating text that contradicts the visual input. Existing research has primarily focused on mitigating object hallucinations, but often overlooks more complex relation hallucinations, particularly action relations involving interactions between objects. In this study, we empirically observe that the primary cause of action-relation hallucinations in LVLMs is the insufficient attention allocated to visual information. Thus, we propose a framework to locate action-relevant image regions and enhance the LVLM's attention to those regions. Specifically, we define the Action-Relation Sensitivity (ARS) score to identify attention heads that are most sensitive to action-relation changes, thereby localizing action-relevant image regions that contain key visual cues. Then, we propose the Relation-aware Visual Enhancement (RVE) method to enhance the LVLM's attention to these action-relevant image regions. Extensive experiments demonstrate that, compared to existing baselines, our method achieves superior performance in mitigating action-relation hallucinations with negligible additional inference cost. Furthermore, it effectively generalizes to spatial-relation hallucinations and object hallucinations.

</details>


### [81] [RevealLayer: Disentangling Hidden and Visible Layers via Occlusion-Aware Image Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11818)
*Binhao Wang,Shihao Zhao,Bo Cheng,Qiuyu Ji,Yuhang Ma,Liebucha Wu,Shanyuan Liu,Dawei Leng,Yuhui Yin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RevealLayer框架，通过区域感知注意力、遮挡引导适配器和复合损失函数，实现自然图像的多层RGBA分解，并构建了RevealLayer-100K数据集支持训练与评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在图像层分解中面临遮挡补全、层解耦鲁棒性差、前景边界不精确等挑战，且缺乏高质量的多层自然图像数据集。

Method: 提出RevealLayer框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 区域感知注意力模块解耦隐藏层与可见层；(2) 遮挡引导适配器利用上下文增强重叠区域；(3) 复合损失强化Alpha边界并抑制伪影。同时构建RevealLayer-100K数据集（自动算法+人工标注）和RevealLayerBench基准。

Result: 在层分解任务上，RevealLayer持续优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 扩散模型结合区域感知注意力、遮挡引导适配器和复合损失，可有效提升自然图像多层分解的性能，高质量数据集构建进一步推动该领域发展。

Abstract: Recent diffusion-based approaches have made substantial progress in image layer decomposition. However, accurately decomposing complex natural images remains challenging due to difficulties in occlusion completion, robust layer disentanglement, and precise foreground boundaries. Moreover, the scarcity of high-quality multi-layer natural image datasets limits advancement. To address these challenges, we propose RevealLayer, a diffusion-based framework that decomposes an RGB image into multiple RGBA layers, enabling precise layer separation and reliable recovery of occluded content in natural images. RevealLayer incorporates three key components: (1) a Region-Aware Attention module to disentangle hidden and visible layers; (2) an Occlusion-Guided Adapter to leverage contextual information to enhance overlapping regions; and (3) a composite loss to enforce sharp alpha boundaries and suppress residual artifacts. To support training and evaluation, we introduce RevealLayer-100K, a high-quality multi-layer natural image constructed through a collaboration between automated algorithms and human annotation, and further establish RevealLayerBench for benchmarking layer decomposition in general natural scenes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RevealLayer consistently outperforms existing approaches in layer decomposition.

</details>


### [82] [REFNet++: Multi-Task Efficient Fusion of Camera and Radar Sensor Data in Bird's-Eye Polar View](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11824)
*Kavin Chandrasekaran,Sorin Grigorescu,Gijs Dubbelman,Pavol Jancura*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于变分编码器-解码器架构的雷达与摄像头多模态融合方法，通过将前视摄像头数据转换到BEV极坐标系、从雷达RD谱恢复角度信息，在统一域中实现高效、鲁棒的传感器融合，并在RADIal数据集上验证了车辆检测和自由空间分割的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 摄像头能提供丰富的视觉信息，但受天气条件影响大；雷达鲁棒性强但噪声大、分类能力弱。两者融合可优势互补，但现有融合方法在精度与计算效率之间难以平衡，且雷达与摄像头数据域不统一，融合困难。

Method: 利用原始距离-多普勒（RD）谱与前视摄像头图像作为输入，采用变分编码器-解码器架构：一个分支将前视摄像头数据转换到鸟瞰图（BEV）极坐标系，另一分支从RD谱恢复角度信息生成距离-方位角（RA）特征，实现两种模态在同一域中对齐，从而进行有效融合。

Result: 在RADIal数据集上针对车辆检测和自由空间分割任务，与当前最优方法相比，所提融合策略在保持计算效率的同时取得了有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: 通过在统一域中对齐雷达和摄像头数据，所提变分编码器-解码器融合架构能够在保证计算效率的前提下实现鲁棒的多模态融合，为实际自动驾驶中的环境感知提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: A realistic view of the vehicle's surroundings is generally offered by camera sensors, which is crucial for environmental perception. Affordable radar sensors, on the other hand, are becoming invaluable due to their robustness in variable weather conditions. However, because of their noisy output and reduced classification capability, they work best when combined with other sensor data. Specifically, we address the challenge of multimodal sensor fusion by aligning radar and camera data in a unified domain, prioritizing not only accuracy, but also computational efficiency. Our work leverages the raw range-Doppler (RD) spectrum from radar and front-view camera images as inputs. To enable effective fusion, we employ a variational encoder-decoder architecture that learns the transformation of front-view camera data into the Bird's-Eye View (BEV) polar domain. Concurrently, a radar encoder-decoder learns to recover the angle information from the RD data that produce Range-Azimuth (RA) features. This alignment ensures that both modalities are represented in a compatible domain, facilitating robust and efficient sensor fusion. We evaluated our fusion strategy for vehicle detection and free space segmentation against state-of-the-art methods using the RADIal dataset.

</details>


### [83] [Selection, Not Fusion: Radar-Modulated State Space Models for Radar-Camera Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11840)
*Zhangcheng Hou,Tomoaki Ohtsuki*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Radar-Modulated Selection (RMS)，将雷达信号注入Mamba的选择性扫描机制内部（调制步长Δ和读出C），而非在扫描外部融合特征。基于此构建SemoDepth模型，在nuScenes数据集上达到SOTA，MAE降低约30-34%，延迟仅26.8ms。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达-相机深度估计方法都在骨干网络的序列算子外部进行特征融合（拼接、门控、图提取等），即便是跨模态Mamba变体也未让雷达参与选择机制本身。作者认为选择机制才是雷达信号进入的正确位置，旨在实现更自然、更高效的跨模态融合。

Method: 提出Radar-Modulated Selection (RMS)：在Mamba的选择性扫描中，用雷达信号从内部调制步长Δ和读出C（零初始化扰动），而输入投影B和状态动力学A保持纯图像。这一构造在初始化时等价于纯图像Mamba，确保雷达只在有益处时影响模型。进一步设计Multi-View Scan Pyramid (MVSP)匹配不同尺度下雷达的空间覆盖范围。

Result: 在nuScenes上达到SOTA：相比此前最佳方法，MAE在0-50m、0-70m、0-80m分别降低34.0%、29.9%、29.9%；单帧延迟最低（26.8ms）。消融实验表明，在RMS基础上额外添加扫描外特征融合不会带来精度提升。

Conclusion: 证明了将雷达信号注入Mamba内部选择机制（而非外部融合）的有效性，实现了线性成本的跨模态循环耦合，且在雷达缺失时可自然退化为纯图像骨干。为多模态深度估计提供了新的设计范式。

Abstract: Radar-camera depth estimation must turn an ultra-sparse, all-weather, metric radar signal into a dense per-pixel depth map. Existing methods -- concatenation, confidence-aware gating, sparse supervision, graph-based extraction -- combine radar and image features outside the backbone's sequence operator, and even cross-modal Mamba variants leave the selection mechanism itself unimodal. We argue that the selection mechanism is the right place for radar to enter. We introduce Radar-Modulated Selection (RMS), a minimal and principled way to inject radar into Mamba's selective scan: radar modulates the scan from within, adding zero-initialised perturbations to the step size $Δ$ and readout $\mathbf{C}$ while leaving the input projection $\mathbf{B}$ and state dynamics $\mathbf{A}$ image-only. The construction is exactly equivalent to a pretrained image-only Mamba at initialisation, ensuring radar only influences the model where it improves accuracy. Two further properties follow that out-of-scan fusion cannot offer: linear-cost cross-modal coupling at every recurrence step, and a natural fallback to the image-only backbone when radar is absent. We deploy RMS in a Multi-View Scan Pyramid (MVSP) that matches the fusion operator to radar's spatial reach at each scale. SemoDepth achieves state-of-the-art performance on nuScenes, reducing MAE by 34.0%, 29.9%, and 29.9% over the previous best at 0--50, 0--70, and 0--80m, while attaining the lowest single-frame latency (26.8ms). A further ablation shows that out-of-scan feature blending adds no accuracy on top of RMS, providing empirical validation that in-scan selection can replace out-of-scan fusion.

</details>


### [84] [UniVLR: Unifying Text and Vision in Visual Latent Reasoning for Multimodal LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11856)
*Houcheng Jiang,Jiajun Fu,Junfeng Fang,Chen Gao,Xiang Wang,Xiangnan He,Yong Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出UniVLR框架，将文本推理和视觉证据统一为视觉工作空间，通过压缩为视觉潜在token实现纯视觉推理，减少推理token数量并提升效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉潜在推理方法仍依赖显式文本思维链与视觉潜在token交错，导致效率低下且推理碎片化，缺乏统一的视觉推理范式。

Method: UniVLR将文本推理痕迹与辅助图像一起渲染为统一的视觉表示，并压缩为紧凑的视觉潜在token；推理时仅通过视觉潜在token进行推理，直接解码最终答案。

Result: 在真实世界感知和视觉推理任务上，UniVLR优于先前视觉潜在推理方法，且使用显著更少的推理token。

Conclusion: UniVLR提出了一种更统一、高效的视觉思维范式，有望推动MLLM在多模态推理中摆脱对显式文本CoT的依赖。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models are increasingly expected to perform thinking with images, yet existing visual latent reasoning methods still rely on explicit textual chain-of-thought interleaved with visual latent tokens. This interleaved design limits efficiency and keeps reasoning fragmented across separate text and vision channels. We propose UniVLR, a unified visual latent reasoning framework that treats textual reasoning and auxiliary visual evidence as a shared visual workspace. Instead of preserving text CoT as an independent inference-time path, UniVLR renders reasoning traces together with auxiliary images and learns to compress this unified representation into compact visual latent tokens. At inference time, the model reasons only through visual latents and directly decodes the final answer, avoiding both external tool calls and verbose text reasoning. Experiments on real-world perception and visual reasoning tasks show that UniVLR outperforms prior visual latent reasoning methods while using substantially fewer generated reasoning tokens, suggesting a more unified and efficient paradigm for visual thinking in MLLMs.

</details>


### [85] [GATA2Floor: Graph attention for floor counting in street-view facades](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11863)
*Ngoc Tan Le,Tzoulio Chamiti,Eirini Papagiannopoulou,Nikos Deligiannis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出GATA2Floor模型，利用图注意力网络对建筑立面进行结构化分析，通过将立面建模为图并引入可学习的跨注意力查询机制，实现无标注数据下的楼层计数和元素分配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建筑立面自动分析在城市分析、能源评估和应急规划中潜力巨大，但现有方法仅关注孤立的目标检测，缺乏对空间布局元素的推理能力。同时，标注数据集的匮乏也限制了相关研究。

Method: 将每个立面建模为以窗户/门检测为节点、以垂直先验为边的图结构。提出基于GATv2的多头图注意力模型GATA2Floor，通过可学习的交叉注意力查询将元素软分配到潜在楼层槽位，从而预测楼层总数。

Result: 利用轻量级无标签提案机制（基于自监督特征和视觉-语言评分）实现无需标注数据的图推理，验证了图注意力关系推理在立面理解中的有效性。

Conclusion: 图注意力机制的关系推理能够有效提升建筑立面理解的性能，且所提方法在缺乏标注数据的情况下仍具备可操作性，为立面自动化分析提供了新思路。

Abstract: Automated analysis of building facades from street-level imagery has great potential for urban analytics, energy assessment, and emergency planning. However, it requires reasoning over spatially arranged elements rather than solely isolated detections. In this work, we model each facade as a graph over window/door detections with a vertical prior on edges. Additionally, we introduce GATA2Floor, a multi-head Graph Attention v2 (GATv2) based model that predicts the global floor count of a building and, via learnable cross-attention queries, softly assigns elements to latent floor slots, yielding interpretable outputs and robustness to irregular designs. To mitigate the lack of labeled datasets, we demonstrate that the proposed graph-based reasoning can be applied without annotations by leveraging a lightweight label-free proposal mechanism based on self-supervised features and vision-language scoring. Our approach demonstrates the value of graph-attention-based relational reasoning for facade understanding.

</details>


### [86] [When Brains Disagree: Biological Ambiguity Underlies the Challenge of Amyloid PET Synthesis from Structural MRI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11867)
*Louise E. G. Baron,Ross Callaghan,David M. Cash,Philip S. Weston,Hojjat Azadbakht,Hui Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过对照实验证明，MRI到淀粉样PET合成的性能受限并非由于模型架构能力不足，而是因为MRI（反映神经退行性变）与PET（反映淀粉样病理）之间存在生物学上的模糊映射关系（一对多映射）。引入多模态信息（如血浆生物标志物）可解决此模糊性，而单纯增加模型复杂度无法带来稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MRI-to-amyloid PET合成研究性能差异大、复杂架构未带来一致性提升，怀疑存在内在生物学模糊性（MRI捕捉神经退行性变，PET测量淀粉样病理，二者在AD中时间上解耦，导致同一MRI模式可能对应不同淀粉样状态）。但该假设尚未被科学验证。

Method: 设计两个控制实验：1）通过按淀粉样和神经退行性状态分层配对MRI-PET数据来控制训练分布，使用两种标准合成模型，比较无歧义数据与引入歧义数据时的性能差异；2）引入正交生物学信息（血浆生物标志物）作为多模态输入，测试能否解决模糊性。

Result: 1）生物学无歧义的映射可被单独学习，但引入数据歧义后性能骤降，证明数据分布歧义而非架构能力限制性能；2）引入多模态输入（血浆生物标志物）后，性能提升且稳定性恢复。

Conclusion: MRI-to-amyloid PET合成性能受限的根本原因是内在生物学模糊性（MRI与淀粉样病理的时间解耦导致一对多映射），而非模型架构能力不足。实现稳定有意义的进展需要多模态信息整合，而非单纯增加架构复杂度。

Abstract: Structural MRI-to-amyloid PET synthesis has been proposed as a non-invasive alternative for amyloid assessment in Alzheimer's disease (AD). However, reported performance of identical models varies widely across studies, and increasingly complex architectures have not led to consistent gains. This inconsistency is thought to be caused by a fundamental biological ambiguity: MRI captures neurodegeneration, while PET measures amyloid pathology - two processes that are often temporally decoupled in AD. As a result, similar MRI patterns may correspond to different amyloid states, creating ambiguous one-to-many mappings. MRI-to-amyloid PET synthesis may therefore be intrinsically ill-posed; however, this idea has yet to be tested scientifically. The aim of this work is to test this hypothesis through two controlled experiments. We first control the training distribution by stratifying paired MRI-PET data by amyloid and neurodegeneration status. Using two standard synthesis models under a controlled design, we show that biologically unambiguous mappings are learnable in isolation, but performance collapses when data ambiguity is introduced. This demonstrates that ambiguity in the data distribution, rather than architectural capacity, constrains performance. Second, we show that introducing orthogonal biological information in the form of plasma biomarkers resolves this ambiguity. When multimodal inputs are incorporated, performance improves and stability is restored. Together, these findings suggest that limited and inconsistent performance in MRI-to-amyloid PET synthesis is explained by intrinsic biological ambiguity, and that stable, meaningful progress requires multimodal integration rather than architectural complexity.

</details>


### [87] [FIS-DiT: Breaking the Few-Step Video Inference Barrier via Training-Free Frame Interleaved Sparsity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11869)
*Jian Tang,Jiawei Fan,Qingbin Liu,Zheng Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出FIS-DiT，一种无需训练、与算子无关的框架，通过帧间交错稀疏性（Frame Interleaved Sparsity）在潜变量帧维度上优化计算，在不进行全规模块计算的情况下刷新所有潜变量位置，在Wan 2.2和HunyuanVideo 1.5上实现2.11–2.41倍加速且性能几乎无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频扩散Transformer（DiTs）加速方法主要利用去噪轨迹上的冗余，但在少步数场景下，时间状态稀缺导致特征复用或预测建模效果有限，形成进一步加速的瓶颈。本文旨在通过将优化焦点从时间轨迹转向潜变量帧维度来突破这一限制。

Method: 提出FIS-DiT框架：核心是帧交错稀疏性（FIS）执行策略，利用潜变量帧维度中固有的帧间稀疏性与结构一致性，在模型层次间交错操作帧子集，以较少计算量刷新所有潜变量位置。该方法无需训练，与具体算子无关。

Result: 在Wan 2.2和HunyuanVideo 1.5上，FIS-DiT实现2.11–2.41倍加速，且VBench-Q和CLIP指标下降可忽略不计。

Conclusion: FIS-DiT为实时高清视频生成提供了一条可扩展且鲁棒的路径，证明了在帧维度上利用稀疏性进行加速的有效性和通用性。

Abstract: While the overall inference latency of Video Diffusion Transformers (DiTs) can be substantially reduced through model distillation, per-step inference latency remains a critical bottleneck. Existing acceleration paradigms primarily exploit redundancy across the denoising trajectory; however, we identify a limitation where these step-wise strategies encounter diminishing returns in few-step regimes. In such scenarios, the scarcity of temporal states prevents effective feature reuse or predictive modeling, creating a formidable barrier to further acceleration. To overcome this, we propose Frame Interleaved Sparsity DiT (FIS-DiT), a training-free and operator-agnostic framework that shifts the optimization focus from the temporal trajectory to the latent frame dimension. Our approach is motivated by an intrinsic duality within this dimension: the existence of frame-wise sparsity that permits reduced computation, coupled with a structural consistency where each frame position remains equally vital to the global spatiotemporal context. Leveraging this insight, we implement Frame Interleaved Sparsity (FIS) as an execution strategy that manipulates frame subsets across the model hierarchy, refreshing all latent positions without requiring full-scale block computation. Empirical evaluations on Wan 2.2 and HunyuanVideo 1.5 demonstrate that FIS-DiT consistently achieves 2.11--2.41$\times$ speedup with negligible degradation across VBench-Q and CLIP metrics, providing a scalable and robust pathway toward real-time high-definition video generation.

</details>


### [88] [$h$-control: Training-Free Camera Control via Block-Conditional Gibbs Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11871)
*Yuzhu Wang,Xi Ye,Duo Su,Yangyang Xu,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出$h$-control方法，通过内循环块条件伪吉布斯精炼和自适应冻结策略，解决了无训练相机控制视频生成中轨迹保持与视觉质量的权衡问题，在多个数据集上取得最优结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有无训练相机控制方法在轨迹遵循和视觉质量之间存在难以平衡的权衡问题，且启发式引导强度调参缺乏鲁棒性。

Method: 提出$h$-control，包含两个核心设计：(1) 在外层硬替换引导步骤中嵌入内循环块条件伪吉布斯精炼，对未观测补集进行同噪声水平的迭代采样，具有理论收敛保证；(2) 利用视频潜变量的条件局部性，将未观测补集划分为3D patches，每个patch由自适应混合指示器跟踪，冻结已收敛的patch以加速高维潜变量收敛。

Result: 在RealEstate10K和DAVIS数据集上，$h$-control在所有七个竞争对手（包括无训练和基于训练的方法）中获得最佳FVD，在所有报告的指标上均优于所有无训练基线方法。

Conclusion: 通过结构性的采样器改进而非启发式调参，有效解决了轨迹保持与视觉质量的矛盾，提供了理论收敛保证和实际加速策略。

Abstract: Training-free camera control for pretrained flow-matching video generators is a partial-observation inverse problem: a depth-warped guidance video supplies noisy evidence on a subset of latent sites, which the sampler must reconcile with the pretrained prior. Existing methods struggle to balance the trade-off between trajectory adherence and visual quality and the heuristic guidance-strength tuning lacks robustness. We propose \textbf{$h$-control}, which resolves this dilemma through a structural change to the sampler: each outer hard-replacement guidance step is augmented with an inner-loop \emph{block-conditional pseudo-Gibbs refinement} on the unobserved complement at the same noise level, with provable convergence to the partial-observation conditional data law. To accelerate convergence on high-dimensional video latents, we exploit their conditional locality, partitioning the unobserved complement into 3D patches, each tracked by a custom mixing indicator that adaptively freezes converged patches. On RealEstate10K and DAVIS, \textbf{$h$-control} attains the best FVD against all seven training-free and training-based competitors, outperforming every training-free baseline on every reported metric.

</details>


### [89] [Learning Subspace-Preserving Sparse Attention Graphs from Heterogeneous Multiview Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11881)
*Jie Chen,Yuanbiao Gou,Chuanbin Liu,Zhu Wang,Xi Peng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SAGL方法，通过双线性注意力分解、动态稀疏门控和α-entmax结构化稀疏投影，从异构多视图数据中学习子空间保持的稀疏注意力图，用于无监督迁移学习，在多个基准数据集上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的无监督迁移学习方法在利用多视图互补信息时，无法忠实地恢复内在的子空间结构，难以构建保持子空间结构的稀疏相似度图以实现跨异构视图的语义对齐。

Method: 1) 双线性注意力分解：捕获高维特征间的非对称相似性，打破传统表示学习的对称瓶颈；2) 动态稀疏门控机制：预测特征特定的压缩因子，自适应控制邻居的拓扑贡献；3) α-entmax结构化稀疏投影：生成子空间保持的稀疏注意力图；4) 基于视图特定图进行稀疏信息聚合，得到判别性表示。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上的大量实验表明，SAGL持续优于最先进的无监督迁移学习方法。

Conclusion: SAGL通过稀疏注意力图学习有效解决了异构多视图数据的子空间保持问题，并为可微分稀疏注意力与概率单纯形约束之间建立了严格的理论联系。

Abstract: The high-dimensional features extracted from large-scale unlabeled data via various pretrained models with diverse architectures are referred to as heterogeneous multiview data. Most existing unsupervised transfer learning methods fail to faithfully recover intrinsic subspace structures when exploiting complementary information across multiple views. Therefore, a fundamental challenge involves constructing sparse similarity graphs that preserve these underlying subspace structures for achieving semantic alignment across heterogeneous views. In this paper, we propose a sparse attention graph learning (SAGL) method that learns subspace-preserving sparse attention graphs from heterogeneous multiview data. Specifically, we introduce a bilinear attention factorization scheme to capture asymmetric similarities among the high-dimensional features, which breaks the symmetry bottleneck that is inherent in the traditional representation learning techniques. A dynamic sparsity gating mechanism then predicts a feature-specific compression factor for adaptively controlling the topological contributions of neighbors. Furthermore, we employ a structured sparse projection via $α$-entmax to generate subspace-preserving sparse attention graphs for individual views. SAGL leverages these view-specific graphs to conduct sparse information aggregation, yielding discriminative representations for multiview learning tasks. In addition, we provide a rigorous theoretical analysis that bridges differentiable sparse attention and probability simplex constraints. Extensive experiments conducted on multiple benchmark datasets demonstrate that SAGL consistently outperforms the state-of-the-art unsupervised transfer learning approaches.

</details>


### [90] [Few-Shot Synthetic Data Generation with Diffusion Models for Downstream Vision Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11898)
*Daniil Dushenev,Nazariy Karpov,Daniil Zinovjev,Alexander Gorin,Konstantin Kulikov*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出使用LoRA微调扩散模型生成少量合成样本（20-50张真实图像），用于增强视觉识别中的罕见类别。在胸部X光病理分类和工业表面裂纹检测两个不同领域的实验中，合成数据增强持续提升了罕见类别的召回率和F1分数，且性能提升随合成比例增加呈现边际递减效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉识别中类别不平衡问题严重，尤其在安全关键领域，罕见事件的正样本收集成本高、数量少，传统方法难以有效学习罕见类别。

Method: 提出轻量级合成数据增强流水线：首先用20-50张真实罕见类图像微调LoRA适配器，再使用预训练扩散模型生成合成样本，并将其与真实数据混合进行训练，系统调节合成与真实样本的比例。

Result: 在两个异构领域（胸部X光病理分类、工业表面裂纹检测）的实验中，合成数据增强一致性地提升了罕见类别的召回率和F1分数；性能随适度合成增强而提升，但合成比例过高时出现边际递减效应。

Conclusion: LoRA适配的扩散模型为罕见类别增强提供了一种简单且可扩展的机制，能够在数据稀缺场景下实现跨异构视觉领域的有效学习。

Abstract: Class imbalance is a persistent challenge in visual recognition, particularly in safety-critical domains where collecting positive examples is expensive and rare events are inherently underrepresented. We propose a lightweight synthetic data augmentation pipeline that fine-tunes a LoRA adapter on as few as 20-50 real images of a rare class and uses a pretrained diffusion model to generate synthetic samples for training.
  We systematically vary the synthetic-to-real ratio and evaluate the approach across two structurally different domains: chest X-ray pathology classification (NIH ChestX-ray14) and industrial surface crack detection (Magnetic Tile Defect dataset). All evaluations are performed on held-out sets of real images only.
  Across both domains, synthetic augmentation consistently improves rare-class recall and F1 compared to training with real data alone. Performance improves with moderate synthetic augmentation and shows diminishing returns as the synthetic ratio increases.
  These results suggest that LoRA-adapted diffusion models provide a simple and scalable mechanism for augmenting rare classes, enabling effective learning in data-scarce scenarios across heterogeneous visual domains.

</details>


### [91] [Mobile Traffic Camera Calibration from Road Geometry for UAV-Based Traffic Surveillance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11900)
*Alexey Popov,Natalia Trukhina,Vadim Vashkelis*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级流水线，将单目倾斜无人机交通视频转换为局部度量鸟瞰图表示，利用可见道路几何估计单应性矩阵，将车辆观测投影到度量地面平面，支持速度、方向、航向和3D立方体估计。在UAVDT数据集上的评估显示有效但存在远场敏感性和单平面假设等局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 原始无人机视频因车辆运动在透视图像坐标中而非稳定度量道路坐标系中，难以直接用于交通分析。现有固定路侧摄像头覆盖不足，无人机虽灵活但缺乏有效的坐标转换方法。

Method: 利用可见道路几何（车道标记、道路边界、人行横道）估计道路平面单应性矩阵，将图像坐标映射到度量地面平面坐标；将车辆检测或标注的观测点通过估计的地面接触点投影到BEV；支持车辆方向、速度、航向和动态3D立方体估计。

Result: 在UAVDT的M1401序列上，40帧样本生成632个度量立方体实例、23条轨迹。道路几何校准可将单目无人机视频转换为类似交通摄像头的可解释分析结果，包括BEV轨迹和同步3D立方体可视化。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了实用的无人机交通摄像头基础，但存在远场车辆对单应性误差敏感、手动校准优于全自动校准、单平面假设限制非平面道路区域性能等局限，可作为未来实时交通数字孪生系统的基础。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) can provide flexible traffic surveillance where fixed roadside cameras are unavailable, costly, or impractical. However, raw UAV video is difficult to use for traffic analytics because vehicle motion is observed in perspective image coordinates rather than in a stable metric road coordinate system. This paper presents a lightweight pipeline for converting monocular oblique UAV traffic video into a local metric bird's-eye-view (BEV) representation. Visible road geometry, including lane markings, road borders, and crosswalks, is used to estimate a road-plane homography from image coordinates to metric ground-plane coordinates. Vehicle observations from dataset annotations or detectors are then projected to BEV using estimated ground contact points. The resulting trajectories support estimation of vehicle direction, speed, heading, and dynamic 3D cuboids on the road plane. We evaluate the pipeline on UAVDT using ground-truth annotations to isolate calibration and geometric reconstruction from detector and tracker errors. For sequence M1401, 40 sampled frames from img000001-img000196 produce 632 metric cuboid instances across 23 tracks. Results show that road-geometry calibration can transform monocular UAV footage into interpretable traffic-camera-style analytics, including BEV tracks and synchronized 3D cuboid visualizations. They also reveal key limitations: far-field vehicles are sensitive to homography errors, manual validation is currently more reliable than fully automatic calibration, and the single-plane assumption limits performance in non-planar or ambiguous road regions. The proposed pipeline provides a practical foundation for deployable UAV traffic cameras and future real-time traffic digital-twin systems.

</details>


### [92] [Beyond Point-wise Neural Collapse: A Topology-Aware Hierarchical Classifier for Class-Incremental Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11904)
*Huiyu Yi,Zhiming Xu,Dunwei Tu,Zhicheng Wang,Baile Xu,Furao Shen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对类增量学习中NCM分类器假设特征坍缩为单点导致次优的问题，提出HC-SOINN分类器（层次聚类SOINN）捕获流形的拓扑结构，并引入STAR方法（结构拓扑对齐残差）通过细粒度点轨迹追踪适应非线性特征漂移，集成到七种SOTA方法中均取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NCM分类器在CIL中因抗遗忘能力强而被广泛使用，但NC理论要求的特征坍缩为单点在CIL中难以实现（非线性特征漂移+训练不足），导致类别呈现复杂流形而非坍缩点，单点NCM是次优的。

Method: 提出HC-SOINN分类器：通过"局部到全局"表征捕获流形的拓扑结构；提出STAR方法：利用细粒度逐点轨迹追踪机制主动变形所学拓扑，以适应非线性特征漂移。

Result: 将HC-SOINN集成到七种SOTA方法的分类器中，均取得一致性改进；理论分析与Procrustes距离实验验证了框架对流形变形的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: HC-SOINN+STAR框架有效解决了NCM在CIL中因特征未坍缩导致的次优问题，通过拓扑流形建模与自适应变形提升了分类性能，鲁棒性强且可广泛集成。

Abstract: The Nearest Class Mean (NCM) classifier is widely favored in Class-Incremental Learning (CIL) for its superior resistance to catastrophic forgetting compared to Fully Connected layers. While Neural Collapse (NC) theory supports NCM's optimality by assuming features collapse into single points, non-linear feature drift and insufficient training in CIL often prevent this ideal state. Consequently, classes manifest as complex manifolds rather than collapsed points, rendering the single-point NCM suboptimal. To address this, we propose Hierarchical-Cluster SOINN (HC-SOINN), a novel classifier that captures the topological structure of these manifolds via a ``local-to-global'' representation. Furthermore, we introduce Structure-Topology Alignment via Residuals (STAR) method, which employs a fine-grained pointwise trajectory tracking mechanism to actively deform the learned topology, allowing it to adapt precisely to complex non-linear feature drift. Theoretical analysis and Procrustes distance experiments validate our framework's resilience to manifold deformations. We integrated HC-SOINN into seven state-of-the-art methods by replacing their original classifiers, achieving consistent improvements that highlight the effectiveness and robustness of our approach. Code is available at https://github.com/yhyet/HC_SOINN.

</details>


### [93] [Vector Scaffolding: Inter-Scale Orchestration for Differentiable Image Vectorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11913)
*Jaerin Lee,Kanggeon Lee,Kyoung Mu Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Vector Scaffolding的分层优化框架，通过解决拓扑坍塌问题，实现了更快、更高质量的矢量图优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可微矢量图方法采用扁平优化，导致拓扑坍塌，产生杂乱无章的"多边形汤"，限制编辑性。

Method: 提出Interior Gradient Aggregation稳定多尺度曲线混合的学习动态，并采用Progressive Stratification和Rapid Inflation Scheduling以极高学习率（×50）渐进加密矢量基元。

Result: 加速优化2.5倍，PSNR提升最高1.4 dB。

Conclusion: 从扁平像素匹配转向结构化拓扑构建，显著提升了矢量图优化的效率和质量。

Abstract: Differentiable vector graphics have enabled powerful gradient-based optimization of vector primitives directly from raster images. However, existing frameworks formulate this as a flat optimization problem, forcing hundreds to thousands of randomly initialized curves to blindly compete for pixel-level error reduction. This disordered optimization leads to topology collapse, where macroscopic structures are distorted by internal high-frequency noise, resulting in a redundant and uneditable "polygon soup" that limits practical editability. To address this limitation, we propose Vector Scaffolding, a novel hierarchical optimization framework that shifts from flat pixel-matching to structured topological construction tailored for vector graphics. By identifying a key cause of topology collapse as the mathematical imbalance between area and boundary gradients, we introduce Interior Gradient Aggregation to stabilize the learning dynamics of multi-scale curve mixtures. Upon this stabilized landscape, we employ Progressive Stratification and Rapid Inflation Scheduling to progressively densify vector primitives with extremely high learning rates ($\times 50$). Experiments demonstrate that our approach accelerates optimization by $2.5\times$ while simultaneously improving PSNR by up to 1.4 dB over the previous state of the art.

</details>


### [94] [RealDiffusion: Physics-informed Attention for Multi-character Storybook Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11927)
*Qi Zhao,Jun Chen,Ivor Tsang,Guang Dai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RealDiffusion是一个统一框架，通过热扩散作为耗散先验来稳定角色身份，并结合区域感知随机过程保持叙事动态性，实现了多角色连贯性与故事动态性的平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型在生成序列图像时难以平衡叙事动态性与多角色连贯性，常导致角色身份丢失或故事停滞。

Method: 提出RealDiffusion框架，包含：1）热扩散作为耗散先验，平均相邻特征并去除高频噪声以抑制属性漂移；2）区域感知随机过程，引入小扰动探索邻近模式防止崩溃；3）轻量级、无需训练的物理约束注意力机制（Physics-informed Attention），在推理时注入可控物理先验到自注意力层。

Result: 大量实验表明RealDiffusion在保持角色连贯性的同时保留了叙事动态性，性能优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: RealDiffusion通过物理启发的注意力机制有效解决了序列生成中连贯性与动态性的权衡问题，且无需额外训练。

Abstract: While modern diffusion models excel at generating diverse single images, extending this to sequential generation reveals a fundamental challenge: balancing narrative dynamism with multi-character coherence. Existing methods often falter at this trade-off, leading to artifacts where characters lose their identity or the story stagnates. To resolve this critical tension, we introduce RealDiffusion, a unified framework designed to reconcile robust coherence with narrative dynamism. Heat diffusion serves as a dissipative prior that averages neighboring features along the sequence and removes high-frequency noise within the subject region. This suppresses attribute drift and stabilizes identity across frames. A region-aware stochastic process then introduces small perturbations that explore nearby modes and prevent collapse so the story maintains pose change and scene evolution. We thus introduce a lightweight, training-free Physics-informed Attention mechanism that injects controllable physical priors into the self-attention layers during inference. By modeling feature evolution as a configurable physical system, our method regularizes spatio-temporal relationships without suppressing intentional, prompt-driven changes. Extensive experiments demonstrate that RealDiffusion achieves substantial gains in character coherence while preserving narrative dynamism, outperforming state-of-the-art approaches. Code is available at https://github.com/ShmilyQi-CN/RealDiffusion.

</details>


### [95] [Learn to Think: Improving Multimodal Reasoning through Vision-Aware Self-Improvement Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11931)
*Qihuang Zhong,Liang Ding,Wenjie Xuan,Juhua Liu,Bo Du,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VISTA是一个视觉感知的自提升训练框架，通过前缀重采样策略和视觉感知注意力评分，解决多模态大语言模型自提升训练中的数据不平衡和语言先验偏差问题，在多种模型和任务上取得显著性能提升（最高+13.66%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型的后训练通常依赖显式推理轨迹，但获取高质量推理轨迹成本高昂。自提升范式虽可让模型自行生成推理轨迹进行训练，但存在两个问题：1) 数据不平衡，简单样本过度训练而困难样本训练不足；2) 语言先验偏差，模型过度依赖语言先验而忽视视觉信息。

Method: 提出VISTA框架，包含两个核心策略：1) 前缀重采样策略（prefix resampling），重用部分正确的推理轨迹以高效收集数据；2) 视觉感知注意力评分（vision-aware attention score），量化模型对视觉信息的关注程度，从而进行针对性训练。

Result: VISTA可应用于监督微调和偏好学习等多种后训练场景，在多个多模态大语言模型和任务上有效提升多模态推理性能，例如在Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct上实现最高+13.66%的平均性能提升。

Conclusion: VISTA通过解决数据不平衡和语言先验偏差问题，有效提升了多模态大语言模型的自提升训练效果，具有广泛的适用性和显著的性能增益。

Abstract: Post-training with explicit reasoning traces is common to improve the reasoning capabilities of Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs). However, acquiring high-quality reasoning traces is often costly and time-consuming. Hence, the self-improvement paradigm has emerged, enabling MLLMs to self-generate reasoning traces for training without external supervision. Despite its effectiveness, we reveal two shortcomings in the self-improvement training of MLLMs: 1) data imbalance, where simple samples are over-trained, but the challenging yet crucial samples are under-trained; 2) language prior bias, where MLLMs overly rely on linguistic priors while neglecting the visual cues. To this end, we propose VISTA, a vision-aware self-improvement training framework for enhancing the multimodal reasoning of MLLMs. Specifically, VISTA first introduces a prefix resampling strategy to reuse the partial correct reasoning traces for efficient data collection, and then designs a vision-aware attention score to quantify the model's focus on visual information. Extensive experiments show that VISTA can be applied to various post-training scenarios, i.e., supervised fine-tuning and preference learning, and effectively enhances the multimodal reasoning performance across various MLLMs and tasks, e.g., bringing up to +13.66% average performance gains for Qwen2.5-VL-3B-Instruct.

</details>


### [96] [Interactive State Space Model with Cross-Modal Local Scanning for Depth Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11934)
*Chen Wu,Ling Wang,Zhuoran Zheng,Xiangyu Chen,Jingyuan Xia,Weidong Jiang,Jiantao Zhou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出基于交互式状态空间模型（Mamba架构）的引导深度超分辨率框架，通过交叉模态局部扫描机制实现RGB和深度特征的细粒度语义交互，在保持线性复杂度的同时达到全局建模效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GDSR方法要么独立建模各模态，要么依赖复杂度为二次的注意力机制，难以建立高效且语义交互的联合表示。

Method: 1) 交叉模态局部扫描机制：实现RGB与深度特征的细粒度语义交互；2) 基于Mamba架构的全局建模（线性复杂度）；3) 交叉模态匹配变换模块：利用代表性特征提升交互建模质量。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上达到与最先进方法竞争的性能。

Conclusion: 提出的交互式状态空间模型框架能够高效实现跨模态密集、语义感知的深层交互，为GDSR任务提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: Guided depth super-resolution (GDSR) reconstructs HR depth maps from LR inputs with HR RGB guidance. Existing methods either model each modality independently or rely on computationally expensive attention mechanisms with quadratic complexity, hindering the establishment of efficient and semantically interactive joint representations. In this paper, we observe that feature maps from different modalities exhibit semantic-level correlations during feature extraction. This motivates us to develop a more flexible approach enabling dense, semantically-aware deep interactions between modalities. To this end, we propose a novel GDSR framework centered around the Interactive State Space Model. Specifically, we design a cross-modal local scanning mechanism that enables fine-grained semantic interactions between RGB and depth features. Leveraging the Mamba architecture, our framework achieves global modeling with linear complexity. Furthermore, a cross-modal matching transform module is introduced to enhance interactive modeling quality by utilizing representative features from both modalities. Extensive experiments demonstrate competitive performance against state-of-the-art methods.

</details>


### [97] [Cluster-Aware Neural Collapse Prompt Tuning for Long-Tailed Generalization of Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11939)
*Boyang Guo,Liang Li,Lin Peng,Yuhan Gao,Xichun Sheng,Chenggang Yan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CPT方法，通过聚类感知的神经坍塌提示学习，增强提示调优VLM在长尾类别上的判别能力，在11个数据集上超越SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有提示学习方法在类别不平衡数据集上难以维持尾部类别的判别能力，导致长尾类性能下降。

Method: 1) 设计聚类不变空间，从预训练VLM挖掘语义分配映射到提示调优特征，计算聚类级边界并限制约束到局部邻域；2) 引入神经坍塌驱动的判别性优化，包含文本ETF分离损失、类内收敛损失和旋转稳定损失三个损失函数，共同塑造类内几何结构以改善类间分离和类内对齐。

Result: 在11个多样化数据集上的实验结果表明，CPT在长尾类上表现更强，对未见类具有良好的泛化能力，总体优于SOTA方法。

Conclusion: CPT通过聚类感知的神经坍塌提示学习有效提升了VLM在长尾分布下的判别能力，且不牺牲整体泛化性能。

Abstract: Prompt learning has emerged as an efficient alternative to fine-tuning pre-trained vision-language models (VLMs). Despite its promise, current methods still struggle to maintain tail-class discriminability when adapting to class-imbalanced datasets. In this work, we propose cluster-aware neural collapse prompt tuning (CPT), which enhances the discriminability of tail classes in prompt-tuned VLMs without sacrificing their overall generalization. First, we design a cluster-invariant space by mining semantic assignments from the pre-trained VLM and mapping them to prompt-tuned features. This computes cluster-level boundaries and restricts the constraints to local neighborhoods, which reduces interference with the global semantic structure of the pre-trained VLM. Second, we introduce neural-collapse-driven discriminability optimization with three losses: textual Equiangular Tight Frame (ETF) separation loss, class-wise convergence loss, and rotation stabilization loss. These losses work together to shape intra-cluster geometry for better inter-class separation and intra-class alignment. Extensive experiments on 11 diverse datasets demonstrate that CPT outperforms SOTA methods, with stronger performance on long-tail classes and good generalization to unseen classes.

</details>


### [98] [Multimodal Abstractive Summarization of Instructional Videos with Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11959)
*Maham Nazir,Muhammad Aqeel,Richong Zhang,Francesco Setti*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: ClipSum利用冻结的CLIP视觉-语言特征进行教学视频摘要，在YouCook2数据集上达到33.0% ROUGE-1，优于ResNet-152的30.5%，且特征维度降低4倍（512 vs 2048）。冻结CLIP（33.0%）优于微调CLIP（32.3%），表明保留预训练对齐比任务特定适应更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视频摘要使用基于物体分类的CNN特征，视觉概念被表示为离散类别，与自然语言语义不对齐，导致视觉-语言之间存在鸿沟。

Method: 提出ClipSum框架，使用冻结的CLIP视觉-语言特征（在4亿图文对上对比预训练），结合显式时间建模和维度自适应融合，用于教学视频摘要。

Result: 在YouCook2上，ClipSum（冻结CLIP）达33.0% ROUGE-1，ResNet-152仅30.5%；冻结CLIP（33.0%）优于微调CLIP（32.3%）；特征维度从2048降至512，性能反而提升。

Conclusion: 语义对齐比特征容量更重要；保留预训练的对齐知识比针对任务微调更有价值；CLIP特征可有效桥接视觉-语言表征层面的鸿沟。

Abstract: Multimodal video summarization requires visual features that align semantically with language generation. Traditional approaches rely on CNN features trained for object classification, which represent visual concepts as discrete categories not aligned with natural language. We propose ClipSum, a framework that leverages frozen CLIP vision-language features with explicit temporal modeling and dimension-adaptive fusion for instructional video summarization. CLIP's contrastive pre-training on 400M image-text pairs yields visual features semantically aligned with the linguistic concepts that text decoders generate, bridging the vision-language gap at the representation level. On YouCook2, ClipSum achieves 33.0% ROUGE-1 versus 30.5% for ResNet-152 with 4x lower dimensionality (512 vs. 2048), demonstrating that semantic alignment matters more than feature capacity. Frozen CLIP (33.0%) surpasses fine-tuned CLIP (32.3%), showing that preserving pre-trained alignment is more valuable than task-specific adaptation. https://github.com/aqeeelmirza/clipsum

</details>


### [99] [Chronicles-OCR: A Cross-Temporal Perception Benchmark for the Evolutionary Trajectory of Chinese Characters](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11960)
*Gengluo Li,Shangpin Peng,Xingyu Wan,Chengquan Zhang,Hao Feng,Xin Xu,Pian Wu,Bang Li,Zengmao Ding,Yongge Liu,Yipei Ye,Yang Yang,Zhan Shu,Guojun Yan,Zhe Li,Can Ma,Weiping Wang,Yu Zhou,Han Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Chronicles-OCR基准，首个专为评估VLLMs跨汉字七种书体演变的视觉感知能力而设计的综合数据集，包含2,800张平衡图像和四项量化任务，揭示了当前模型在跨时间文字分布漂移下的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLLMs在文本丰富视觉理解中表现优异，但其对历史文字系统连续形态演变的感知鲁棒性尚未被探索；现有古文字数据集局限于孤立历史时期，无法反映跨越数千年的系统性视觉分布偏移。

Method: 与顶级机构专家合作，构建了包含2,800张跨七种汉字书体、多种物理介质（龟甲到纸本书法）的平衡数据集，提出阶段自适应标注范式，并设计了跨时期字符定位、细粒度古文字识别、古代文本解析、书体分类四项任务。

Result: Chronicles-OCR将视觉感知与语义推理分离，提供了权威平台来暴露当前VLLMs的局限性，为鲁棒的、可感知演化的历史文本感知铺平道路。

Conclusion: 当前VLLMs在跨时序历史文字感知上存在显著不足，Chronicles-OCR为数字人文领域提供了评估和改进此类能力的标准化基准。

Abstract: Vision Large Language Models (VLLMs) have achieved remarkable success in modern text-rich visual understanding. However, their perceptual robustness in the face of the continuous morphological evolution of historical writing systems remains largely unexplored. Existing ancient text datasets typically focus on isolated historical periods, failing to capture the systematic visual distribution shifts spanning thousands of years. To bridge this gap and empower Digital Humanities, we introduce Chronicles-OCR, the first comprehensive benchmark specifically designed to evaluate the cross-temporal visual perception capabilities of VLLMs across the complete evolutionary trajectory of Chinese characters, known as the Seven Chinese Scripts. Curated in collaboration with top-tier institutional domain experts, the dataset comprises 2,800 strictly balanced images encompassing highly diverse physical media, ranging from tortoise shells to paper-based calligraphy. To accommodate the drastic morphological and topological variations across different historical stages, we propose a novel Stage-Adaptive Annotation Paradigm. Based on this, Chronicles-OCR formulates four rigorous quantitative tasks: cross-period character spotting, fine-grained archaic character recognition via visual referring, ancient text parsing, and script classification. By isolating visual perception from semantic reasoning, Chronicles-OCR provides an authoritative platform to expose the limitations of current VLLMs, paving the way for robust, evolution-aware historical text perception. Chronicles-OCR is publicly available at https://github.com/VirtualLUOUCAS/Chronicles-OCR.

</details>


### [100] [What Does It Mean for a Medical AI System to Be Right?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11963)
*Antony Gitau*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以多发性骨髓瘤诊断中浆细胞自动分类为具体案例，论证医疗AI系统的“正确性”是一个多维概念，涉及标注数据质量、模型可解释性、评估指标的临床意义以及人机协作中的责任分配，而非单一的基准性能指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将医疗AI的正确性简化为基准测试性能，忽视了临床实践中正确性的复杂维度。作者希望从科学哲学和研究伦理的角度，重新审视医疗AI“正确”意味着什么。

Method: 以多发性骨髓瘤诊断中的浆细胞自动分类为具体临床案例，通过四个相互关联的主题（真实标签的不稳定性、过度自信AI的不透明性、标准临床指标的不足、时间压力下的自动化偏误风险）展开论证，融合科学哲学与研究伦理的分析框架。

Result: 论证了医疗AI的正确性是包含数据集标注质量、模型可解释性、评估指标临床意义和人类-AI工作流责任分配的多维概念。

Conclusion: 医疗AI系统的“正确性”不能被简化为基准性能，而需要从多维度进行综合评估，这对临床AI的负责任部署具有重要指导意义。

Abstract: This paper examines what it means for a medical AI system to be right by grounding the question in a specific clinical context: the automatic classification of plasma cells in digitized bone marrow smears for the diagnosis of multiple myeloma. Drawing on philosophy of science and research ethics, the paper argues that correctness in medical AI is not a singular property reducible to benchmark performance, but a multi-dimensional concept involving the availability of expertly labeled medical datasets, the explainability and interpretability of model outputs, the clinical meaningfulness of evaluation metrics, and the distribution of accountability in human-AI workflows. As such, the paper develops this argument through four interrelated themes: the instability of ground truth labels, the opacity of overconfident AI, the inadequacy of standard clinical metrics, and the risk of automation bias in time-pressured clinical settings.

</details>


### [101] [H2G: Hierarchy-Aware Hyperbolic Grouping for 3D Scenes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11967)
*ByungHa Ko,Youngmin Lee,Dong Hwan Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: H2G通过双曲仿射场将2D基础模型线索转化为3D层次分组，在无需语义标签的情况下实现多粒度场景分组。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D分组方法依赖语义标签或固定词汇，无法灵活地跨粒度（从部件到完整物体）进行层次化分组，且缺乏将2D基础模型知识有效迁移到3D表示的监督机制。

Method: 提出H2G框架：1）利用Dasgupta目标函数从基础模型亲和度中推导语义树监督；2）将监督蒸馏到单一Lorentz双曲特征场中，利用其几何特性适配树状分支结构；3）设计层次感知目标函数，联合优化细粒度分配、粗粒度物体结构、紧凑特征聚类和LCA排序。

Result: 在单一特征空间中同时表示多个分组层次，实现了基于2D基础模型知识的语义层次化3D分组，无需预定义标签或词汇。

Conclusion: H2G有效桥接了2D基础模型与3D层次分组，证明了双曲几何在编码层次结构方面的优势，为无监督3D场景理解提供了新范式。

Abstract: Hierarchical 3D grouping aims to recover scene groups across multiple granularities, from fine object parts to complete objects, without relying on semantic labels or a fixed vocabulary. The main challenge is to transform 2D foundation-model cues into coherent hierarchy supervision and embed that hierarchy in a 3D representation. We propose H2G, a hyperbolic affinity field for hierarchical 3D grouping. Our method derives semantically organized tree supervision by interpreting foundation-model affinities through Dasgupta's objective for similarity-based hierarchical clustering. This supervision is distilled into a single Lorentz hyperbolic feature field, whose geometry is well suited for tree-like branching structures. A hierarchy-aware objective aligns the field with fine-level assignments, coarse object structure, compact feature clusters, and LCA (Lowest Common Ancestor) ordering. This formulation represents multiple grouping levels in one feature space, enabling semantic hierarchical grouping grounded in 2D foundation-model knowledge.

</details>


### [102] [Optimizing 4D Wires for Sparse 3D Abstraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11977)
*Dong-Yi Wu,Tong-Yee Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种用单一连续4D样条曲线(空间坐标+宽度)进行3D几何抽象的统一框架，将3D草图从局部密度累积转变为全局路由问题，支持基于梯度的优化，在图像到3D抽象、多视角线艺生成等任务中优于离散曲线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法用多条独立曲线段表示3D形状，导致结构碎片化、物理可实现性差，缺乏全局拓扑连贯性。

Method: 使用单一连续4D B样条曲线(x,y,z,w)表示3D形状，将3D草图构建转化为全局路由问题；引入可微分渲染管线实现变宽曲线的高效栅格化，支持SDS或CLIP等梯度引导优化。

Result: 在图像到3D抽象、多视角线艺生成、可微分风格化曲面填充等任务中，相比离散曲线方法，本方法生成的结构具有更高的语义保真度和结构连贯性。

Conclusion: 单一连续样条曲线足以表达复杂体积形式，通过连续性约束提供了更干净美学和更好结构连贯性的归纳偏置，为3D几何抽象提供了一个统一高效的框架。

Abstract: We present a unified framework for 3D geometric abstraction using a single continuous 4D wire, parameterized as a B-spline with spatial coordinates and variable width $(x,y,z,w)$. Existing approaches typically represent shapes as collections of many independent curve segments, which often leads to fragmented structures and limited physical realizability. In contrast, we show that a single continuous spline is sufficiently expressive to capture complex volumetric forms while enforcing global topological coherence. By imposing continuity, our method transforms 3D sketching from a local density-accumulation process into a global routing problem, providing a strong inductive bias toward cleaner aesthetics and improved structural coherence. To enable gradient-based optimization, we introduce a differentiable rendering pipeline that efficiently rasterizes variable-width curves with bounded projection error. This formulation supports robust optimization using modern guidance signals such as Score Distillation Sampling (SDS) or CLIP. We demonstrate applications including image-to-3D abstraction, multi-view wire art generation, and differentiable stylized surface filling. Experiments show that our unified representation produces structures with higher semantic fidelity and improved structural coherence compared to approaches based on collections of discrete curves.

</details>


### [103] [A Transfer Learning Evaluation of Deep Neural Networks for Image Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11989)
*Nermeen Abou Baker,Nico Zengeler,Uwe Handmann*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了在图像分类任务中如何选择最佳的预训练模型进行迁移学习，通过在五个目标域数据集上测试十一个ImageNet预训练模型，评估了准确率、准确率密度、训练时间和模型大小等指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 迁移学习能节省训练时间、内存和网络设计成本，但如何为特定目标域选择最佳预训练模型是一个关键问题。

Method: 选取11个ImageNet预训练模型，调整输出层和网络参数，在5个不同目标域数据集上进行迁移学习实验，分别进行单次训练和十次训练，测量准确率、准确率密度、训练时间和模型大小。

Result: 通过多维度评估指标（准确率、准确率密度、训练时间、模型大小）对比了不同预训练模型在不同目标域上的迁移效果。

Conclusion: 为图像分类任务中选择合适的预训练模型提供了系统的评估框架和实验依据。

Abstract: Transfer learning is a machine learning technique that uses previously acquired knowledge from a source domain to enhance learning in a target domain by reusing learned weights. This technique is ubiquitous because of its great advantages in achieving high performance while saving training time, memory, and effort in network design. In this paper, we investigate how to select the best pre-trained model that meets the target domain requirements for image classification tasks. In our study, we refined the output layers and general network parameters to apply the knowledge of eleven image processing models, pre-trained on ImageNet, to five different target domain datasets. We measured the accuracy, accuracy density, training time, and model size to evaluate the pre-trained models both in training sessions in one episode and with ten episodes.

</details>


### [104] [EDGER: EDge-Guided with HEatmap Refinement for Generalizable Image Forgery Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12002)
*Minh-Khoa Le-Phan,Minh-Hoang Le,Minh-Triet Tran,Trong-Le Do*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出EDGER，一个基于双分支框架的图像伪造区域定位方法，结合边缘引导分割和合成热力图分支，实现对任意分辨率图像中篡改区域的精准定位，并在MediaEval 2025 SynthIM挑战中展现了强跨领域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 文本引导的修复技术使图像伪造越来越逼真，现有方法难以跨领域有效检测可疑信号，亟需一种能适应任意分辨率且具备良好泛化能力的伪造定位方法。

Method: 提出双分支框架EDGER：(1) Edge-Guided Segmentation分支，使用基于频率的边缘检测器突出篡改边界的高频不一致性，结合SegFormer融合RGB与边缘特征生成像素级掩码；(2) Synthetic Heatmapping分支，基于分类思想微调CLIP-ViT图像编码器（使用LoRA），对全合成补丁进行标记，提供粗粒度的补丁级合成先验。两分支互补，共实现精确定位。

Result: 在MediaEval 2025 SynthIM挑战的篡改区域定位任务中评估，该方法可扩展到多百万像素级别图像，并展现出强跨领域泛化能力。大量消融实验验证了频率边缘线索与补丁级合成先验的互补作用。

Conclusion: EDGER通过双分支互补策略（边缘级+补丁级）有效解决了跨域伪造定位问题，在不牺牲原生分辨率的前提下实现了对任意分辨率图像中篡改区域的精准定位，为图像伪造检测提供了新的解决思路。

Abstract: Text-guided inpainting has made image forgery increasingly realistic, challenging both SID and IFL. However, existing methods often struggle to point out suspicious signals across domains. To address this problem, we propose EDGER, a patch-based, dual-branch framework that localizes manipulated regions in arbitrary resolution images without sacrificing native resolution. The first branch, Edge-Guided Segmentation, introduces a Frequency-based Edge Detector to emphasize high-frequency inconsistencies at manipulation boundaries, and fine-tunes a SegFormer to fuse RGB and edge features for pixel-level masks. Since edge evidence is most informative only when patches contain both authentic and manipulated pixels, we complement Edge-Guided Segmentation with a Synthetic Heatmapping branch, a classification-based localizer that fine-tunes a CLIP-ViT image encoder with LoRA to flag fully synthetic patches. Together, Synthetic Heatmapping provides coarse, patch-level synthetic priors, while Edge-Guided Segmentation sharpens boundaries within partially manipulated patches, yielding comprehensive localization. Evaluated in the MediaEval 2025, SynthIM challenge, Manipulated Region Localization Task's setting, our approach scales to multi-megapixel imagery and exhibits strong cross-domain generalization. Extensive ablations highlight the complementary roles of frequency-based edge cues and patch-level synthetic priors in driving accurate, resolution-agnostic localization.

</details>


### [105] [Robust Promptable Video Object Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12006)
*Sohyun Lee,Yeho Gwon,Lukas Hoyer,Konrad Schindler,Christos Sakaridis,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次系统研究鲁棒性提示视频目标分割（RobustPVOS），构建了含真实世界恶劣条件与合成腐败数据的综合基准，并提出MoGA方法，通过记忆目标条件门控秩适配机制，有效处理目标特异性退化并保持时序一致性，显著提升模型鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PVOS模型在输入腐败（恶劣天气、噪声等）下性能严重下降，阻碍其在安全关键领域（如自动驾驶、视频监控）的部署，而该领域缺乏系统性鲁棒性研究。

Method: 提出Memory-object-conditioned Gated-rank Adaptation (MoGA) 方法，核心两点：1) 利用跨帧记忆中的目标特定表示来条件化鲁棒化过程，使模型对不同跟踪目标差异化处理；2) 确保时序一致性。同时构建包含真实世界数据（351个视频片段、2500+掩码）和合成腐败训练数据的综合基准。

Result: 在合成和真实世界数据集上，MoGA在多种腐败类型下均取得一致且显著的性能提升，验证了其有效性，为未来RobustPVOS研究建立了强基线。

Conclusion: MoGA通过目标感知的鲁棒化与时序一致性机制有效解决了PVOS在恶劣条件下的退化问题，基准和代码已公开，推动该方向后续研究。

Abstract: The performance of promptable video object segmentation (PVOS) models substantially degrades under input corruptions, which prevents PVOS deployment in safety-critical domains. This paper offers the first comprehensive study on robust PVOS (RobustPVOS). We first construct a new, comprehensive benchmark with two real-world evaluation datasets of 351 video clips and more than 2,500 object masks under real-world adverse conditions. At the same time, we generate synthetic training data by applying diverse and temporally varying corruptions to existing VOS datasets. Moreover, we present a new RobustPVOS method, dubbed Memory-object-conditioned Gated-rank Adaptation (MoGA). The key to successfully performing RobustPVOS is two-fold: effectively handling object-specific degradation and ensuring temporal consistency in predictions. MoGA leverages object-specific representations maintained in memory across frames to condition the robustification process, which allows the model to handle each tracked object differently in a temporally consistent way. Extensive experiments on our benchmark validate MoGA's efficacy, showing consistent and significant improvements across diverse corruption types on both synthetic and real-world datasets, establishing a strong baseline for future RobustPVOS research. Our benchmark is publicly available at https://sohyun-l.github.io/RobustPVOS_project_page/.

</details>


### [106] [L2P: Unlocking Latent Potential for Pixel Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12013)
*Zhennan Chen,Junwei Zhu,Xu Chen,Jiangning Zhang,Jiawei Chen,Zhuoqi Zeng,Wei Zhang,Chengjie Wang,Jian Yang,Ying Tai*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出L2P迁移范式，利用预训练LDM的中间层，仅训练浅层进行latent-to-pixel变换，用合成图像微调，实现零真实数据、高效率的像素级扩散模型训练，支持4K分辨率生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从头训练像素扩散模型需要巨大的计算和数据资源，而现有LDM已蕴含丰富知识，如何高效迁移这些先验到像素空间是核心动机。

Method: 舍弃VAE，改用大块token化；冻结预训练LDM的中间层，仅训练浅层学习latent-to-pixel变换；仅使用LDM生成的合成图像作为训练数据。

Result: 仅需8块GPU训练，在DPG-Bench上与原LDM持平，在GenEval上达到93%性能；支持原生4K超高清分辨率生成。

Conclusion: L2P是一种高效、低成本、零真实数据依赖的迁移方法，能将LDM的先验知识无缝迁移到像素空间，同时解锁超高分辨率生成能力。

Abstract: Pixel diffusion models have recently regained attention for visual generation. However, training advanced pixel-space models from scratch demands prohibitive computational and data resources. To address this, we propose the Latent-to-Pixel (L2P) transfer paradigm, an efficient framework that directly harnesses the rich knowledge of pre-trained LDMs to build powerful pixel-space models. Specifically, L2P discards the VAE in favor of large-patch tokenization and freezes the source LDM's intermediate layers, exclusively training shallow layers to learn the latent-to-pixel transformation. By utilizing LDM-generated synthetic images as the sole training corpus, L2P fits an already smooth data manifold, enabling rapid convergence with zero real-data collection. This strategy allows L2P to seamlessly migrate massive latent priors to the pixel space using only 8 GPUs. Furthermore, eliminating the VAE memory bottleneck unlocks native 4K ultra-high resolution generation. Extensive experiments across mainstream LDM architectures show that L2P incurs negligible training overhead, yet performs on par with the source LDM on DPG-Bench and reaches 93% performance on GenEval.

</details>


### [107] [FAME: Feature Activation Map Explanation on Image Classification and Face Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12017)
*Xinyi Zhang,Manuel Günther*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FAME方法，结合类激活映射与扰动方法的优势，利用网络梯度以梯度驱动方式而非固定块方式扰动输入图像，生成更具可解释性的归因图。实验表明深层网络中CAM的假设不成立，FAME在图像分类和人脸识别任务上达到SOTA水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型在图像处理中精度极高但可解释性差，现有CAM类方法假设特征图位置仅受底层区域影响（该假设在深层网络中不成立），而扰动方法如CorrRISE用固定块扰动不够灵活。需要一种结合两者优势、更精准的解释方法。

Method: 提出FAME方法，利用网络梯度计算对输入图像的扰动，以梯度驱动的方式（而非固定块）操纵输入，结合CAM的特征图信息和扰动方法的灵活性生成归因图。

Result: 在图像分类和人脸识别任务上，定性定量实验表明FAME生成的归因图优于或达到SOTA水平，同时验证了CAM的局部性假设在深层网络中不成立。

Conclusion: FAME有效融合了CAM和扰动方法的优势，为深层网络提供更准确的解释，代码已开源。

Abstract: Deep Learning has revolutionized machine learning, reaching unprecedented levels of accuracy, but at the cost of reduced interpretability. Especially in image processing systems, deep networks transform local pixel information into more global concepts in a highly obscured manner. Explainable AI methods for image processing try to shed light on this issue by highlighting the regions of the image that are important for the prediction task. Among these, Class Activation Mapping (CAM) and its gradient-based variants compute attributions based on the feature map and upscale them to the image resolution, assuming that feature map locations are influenced only by underlying regions. Perturbation-based methods, such as CorrRISE, on the other hand, try to provide pixel-level attributions by perturbing the input with fixed patches and checking how the output of the network changes. In this work, we propose Feature Activation Map Explanation (FAME), which combines both worlds by using network gradients to compute changes to the input image, manipulating it in a gradient-driven way rather than using fixed patches. We apply this technique on two common tasks, image classification and face recognition, and show that CAM's above-mentioned assumption does not hold for deeper networks. We qualitatively and quantitively show that FAME produces attribution maps that are competitive state-of-the-art systems. Our code is available: {\footnotesize https://github.com/AIML-IfI/fame.}

</details>


### [108] [What-Where Transformer: A Slot-Centric Visual Backbone for Concurrent Representation and Localization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12021)
*Ryota Yoshihashi,Masahiro Kada,Satoshi Ikehata,Rei Kawakami,Ikuro Sato*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出What-Where Transformer (WWT)，通过将token视为"what"表示、注意力图视为"where"表示，在Vision Transformer中引入what-where分离的归纳偏置，使得模型在仅使用ImageNet分类监督下就能涌现出多目标发现能力，并在零样本目标发现和弱监督语义分割任务上取得优于ViT的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分类导向的骨干网络倾向于强调"是什么"的语义信息，同时隐含地纠缠或抑制了"在哪里"的位置信息，导致目标发现、检测、分割等定位任务比分类任务复杂得多。

Method: 提出WWT：(1) 将tokens视为"what"表示，注意力图视为"where"表示，通过多流、基于slot的架构在并行前馈模块中处理；(2) 复用最终的tokens和注意力图用于下游任务，直接暴露于任务损失的梯度，促进定位信息的有效学习。

Result: 在仅使用ImageNet单标签分类监督下，WWT能从原始注意力图中直接涌现出多目标发现能力，无需token聚类等后处理；在零样本目标发现和弱监督语义分割任务上优于ViT方法，且可迁移至多种定位设置。

Conclusion: 通过显式分离"what"和"where"的归纳偏置，WWT使得分类骨干网络同时具备强大的语义理解和定位能力，为统一感知与定位提供了新思路。

Abstract: Many image understanding tasks involve identifying what is present and where it appears. However, tasks that address where, such as object discovery, detection, and segmentation, are often considerably more complex than image classification, which primarily focuses on what. One possible reason is that classification-oriented backbones tend to emphasize semantic information about what, while implicitly entangling or suppressing information about where. In this work, we focus on an inductive bias termed what-where separation, which encourages models to represent object appearance and spatial location in a decomposed manner. To incorporate this bias throughout an attentive backbone in the style of Vision Transformer (ViT), we propose the What-Where Transformer (WWT). Our method introduces two key novel designs: (1) it treats tokens as representations of what and attention maps as representations of where, and processes them in concurrent feed-forward modules via a multi-stream, slot-based architecture; (2) it reuses both the final-layer tokens and attention maps for downstream tasks, and directly exposes them to gradients derived from task losses, thereby facilitating more effective and explicit learning of localization. We demonstrate that even under standard single-label classification-based supervision on ImageNet, WWT exhibits emergent multiple object discovery directly from raw attention maps, rather than via additional postprocessing such as token clustering. Furthermore, WWT achieves superior performance compared to ViT-based methods on zero-shot object discovery and weakly supervised semantic segmentation, and it is transferable to various localization setups with minimal modifications. Code will be published after acceptance.

</details>


### [109] [Spectral Vision Transformer for Efficient Tokenization with Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12026)
*Alexandra G. Roberts,Maneesh John,Jinwei Zhang,Dominick Romano,Mert Sisman,Ki Sueng Choi,Heejong Kim,Mert R. Sabuncu,Thanh D. Nguyen,Alexey V. Dimov,Pascal Spincemaille,Brian H. Kopell,Yi Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种频谱视觉Transformer架构，通过频谱投影降低复杂度，在有限数据（尤其是医学影像）上实现高效token化，具有空间不变性和最优信噪比等理论优势，参数量更少但性能优于多种主流模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉Transformer在医学影像等小样本数据上存在token化效率低、计算复杂度高的问题，需要更高效的架构来处理有限数据下的图像识别任务。

Method: 提出频谱视觉Transformer（Spectral ViT），利用频谱投影代替传统空间token化方法，通过选择特定基函数实现空间不变性和最优信噪比，从而降低计算复杂度并提升数据效率。

Result: 在模拟数据、公开数据集和临床数据上，该方法以更少的参数量达到了与紧凑型/标准视觉Transformer、带注意力的CNN、Swin Transformer、MLP乃至逻辑回归等模型相当或更优的性能。

Conclusion: 频谱视觉Transformer在有限数据场景下是一种高效且理论完备的替代方案，尤其适用于医学影像分析，且代码已开源。

Abstract: We propose a novel spectral vision transformer architecture for efficient tokenization in limited data, with an emphasis on medical imaging. We outline convenient theoretical properties arising from the choice of basis including spatial invariance and optimal signal-to-noise ratio. We show reduced complexity arising from the spectral projection compared to spatial vision transformers. We show equitable or superior performance with a reduced number of parameters as compared to a variety of models including compact and standard vision transformers, convolutional neural networks with attention, shifted window transformers, multi-layer perceptrons, and logistic regression. We include simulated, public, and clinical data in our analysis and release our code at: \verb+github.com/agr78/spectralViT+.

</details>


### [110] [4DVGGT-D: 4D Visual Geometry Transformer with Improved Dynamic Depth Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12027)
*Ying Zang,Xuanyi Liu,Yidong Han,Deyi Ji,Chaotao Ding,Yuanqi Hu,Qi Zhu,Xuanfu Li,Jin Ma,Lingyun Sun,Tianrun Chen,Lanyun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需训练、渐进式解耦框架，通过动力学掩码引导的姿态解耦、拓扑子空间手术和信息论置信度融合三个模块，从单目视频中稳健重建动态4D场景，在标准基准上取得显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3D基础模型在动态环境中性能严重退化，根源在于全局注意力机制中相机自身运动与物体运动内在耦合，亟需一种有效解耦动态与静态的方法。
