In addition, even existing toxicity repair benchmarks suffer from limited data diversity, low structural validity of generated molecules, and heavy reliance on proxy models for toxicity assessment. To address these limitations, we propose MolDeTox, a novel benchmark for molecular detoxification, designed to enable fine-grained and reliable evaluation of toxicity-aware molecular optimization across stepwise tasks. We evaluate a wide range of general-purpose LLMs and VLMs under diverse settings, and demonstrate that understanding and generating molecules at the fragment-level improves structural validity and enhances the quality of generated molecules. Moreover, through detailed task-level performance analysis, MolDeTox provides an interpretable benchmark that enables a deeper understanding of the detoxification process. Our dataset is available at : https://huggingface.co/datasets/MolDeTox/MolDeTox

</details>


### [251] [Goal-Oriented Reasoning for RAG-based Memory in Conversational Agentic LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12213)
*Jiazhou Liang,Armin Toroghi,Yifan Simon Liu,Faeze Moradi Kalarde,Liam Gallagher,Scott Sanner*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出Goal-Mem框架，通过将用户话语作为目标进行显式反向链接推理，实现RAG式代理记忆中的目标导向推理，在需要多跳推理和隐式推断的任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于RAG的记忆方法通常根据原始用户话语的语义相似性进行检索，缺乏对缺失中间事实的显式推理，检索到的证据往往不相关或不足以支持基于推理的回答。

Method: 提出Goal-Mem框架：将用户话语作为目标，执行显式反向链接推理，将每个目标分解为原子子目标，进行针对性记忆检索以满足每个子目标，并在中间目标无法解决时迭代识别应从记忆中检索什么信息。该方法基于自然语言逻辑（Natural Language Logic）形式化，结合FOL的可验证推理和自然语言的表达能力。

Result: 在两个数据集上与九个强记忆基线方法对比，Goal-Mem在性能上持续提升，尤其在需要多跳推理和隐式推断的任务上表现突出。

Conclusion: Goal-Mem通过显式目标分解和反向链接推理，有效克服了传统RAG记忆方法在复杂推理任务上的局限性，为对话代理的长程记忆推理提供了新的解决方案。

Abstract: LLM-based conversational AI agents struggle to maintain coherent behavior over long horizons due to limited context. While RAG-based approaches are increasingly adopted to overcome this limitation by storing interactions in external memory modules and performing retrieval from them, their effectiveness in answering challenging questions (e.g., multi-hop, commonsense) ultimately depends on the agent's ability to reason over the retrieved information. However, existing methods typically retrieve memory based on semantic similarity to the raw user utterance, which lacks explicit reasoning about missing intermediate facts and often returns evidence that is irrelevant or insufficient for grounded reasoning. In this work, we introduce Goal-Mem, a goal-oriented reasoning framework for RAG-based agentic memory that performs explicit backward chaining from the user's utterance as a goal. Rather than progressively expanding from retrieved context, Goal-Mem decomposes each goal into atomic subgoals, performs targeted memory retrieval to satisfy each subgoal, and iteratively identifies what information from memory should be retrieved when intermediate goals cannot be resolved. We formalize this process in Natural Language Logic, a logical system that combines the verifiability of reasoning provided by FOL with the expressivity of natural language. Through extensive experiments on two datasets and comparing to nine strong memory baselines, we show that Goal-Mem consistently improves performance, particularly on tasks requiring multi-hop reasoning and implicit inference.

</details>


### [252] [No Action Without a NOD: A Heterogeneous Multi-Agent Architecture for Reliable Service Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12240)
*Zixu Yang,Hang Zheng,Nan Jiang,Zhiyang Tang,Situo Zhang,Xiaobao Wu,Lu Chen,Kai Yu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种异构多智能体架构NOD（导航器-操作器-导演），通过外部化全局状态和选择性外部监督，提升服务型LLM智能体在长周期任务中的可靠性，减少策略违规、工具幻觉和意图偏离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM服务智能体在长周期任务中不可靠，常出现策略违规、工具幻觉和动作对齐错误，阻碍实际部署。

Method: 采用异构多智能体架构NOD，包含Navigator（维护结构化全局状态进行显式任务跟踪）、Operator（执行动作）和Director（在关键动作前进行独立监督和干预）。

Result: 在τ²-Bench上，NOD在任务成功率和关键动作精度上优于基线，同时减少了策略违规、工具幻觉和用户意图偏离。

Conclusion: 通过外部化状态跟踪和选择性监督，NOD有效提升了服务智能体在长周期任务中的可靠性和安全性。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM) agents have increasingly advanced service applications, such as booking flight tickets. However, these service agents suffer from unreliability in long-horizon tasks, as they often produce policy violations, tool hallucinations, and misaligned actions, which greatly impedes their real-world deployment. To address these challenges, we propose NOD (Navigator-Operator-Director), a heterogeneous multi-agent architecture for service agents. Instead of maintaining task state implicitly in dialogue context as in prior work, we externalize a structured Global State to enable explicit task state tracking and consistent decision-making by the Navigator. Besides, we introduce selective external oversight before critical actions, allowing an independent Director agent to verify execution and intervene when necessary. As such, NOD effectively mitigates error propagation and unsafe behavior in long-horizon tasks. Experiments on $τ^2$-Bench demonstrate that NOD achieves higher task success rates and critical action precision over baselines. More importantly, NOD improves the reliability of service agents by reducing policy violations, tool hallucinations, and user-intent misalignment.

</details>


### [253] [Why Conclusions Diverge from the Same Observations: Formalizing World-Model Non-Identifiability via an Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12255)
*Toru Takahashi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出，人们基于相同证据得出不同结论并非源于认知缺陷，而是推理与学习中固有的"不可识别性"问题，并构建了θ级和W级两层分析框架，用推理画像（参考、探索、稳定、视域）解释分歧产生机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 挑战"分歧即非理性/认知缺陷"的常见判断，主张分歧是学习系统固有的非可识别性问题。

Method: 提出两层非可识别性框架：θ级（相同世界模型下因推理设置不同导致分歧）和W级（推理设置反复使用导致世界模型本身分化）。引入推理画像θ=(R,E,S,D)四元组，解释相同观测和世界模型下输出为何分裂。

Result: 解释了分歧为何倾向于投射到少数基轴上（抽象vs具体、外部化、秩序vs自由），归因于学习系统的三类约束（计算、观测、协调约束）。将框架与深度表征学习（表征层级、隐状态估计、正则化-探索权衡）相联系，并通过AI监管辩论案例验证。

Conclusion: 分歧应被视为推理系统内在的不可识别性现象，而非他人的认知缺陷；该框架为理解、预测和应对分歧提供了理论基础。

Abstract: When people share the same documents and observations yet reach different conclusions, the disagreement often shifts into a judgment that the other party is cognitively defective, irrational, or acting in bad faith. This paper argues that such divergence is better described as a form of non-identifiability inherent in inference and learning, rather than as a defect of the other party. We organize the phenomenon into two levels: (i) $θ$-level non-identifiability, where conclusions diverge under the same world model $W$ because inference settings differ; and (ii) $W$-level non-identifiability, where repeated use of an inference setting $θ$ biases data exposure and update rules, causing the learned world model $W$ itself to diverge. We introduce an inference profile $θ= (R, E, S, D)$, consisting of Reference, Exploration, Stabilization, and Horizon, and show how outputs can split even for the same observation $o$ and the same $W$. We further explain why disagreements tend to project onto a small number of bases -- abstract versus concrete, externalizability, and order versus freedom -- as a consequence of general constraints on learning systems: computational, observational, and coordination constraints. Finally, we relate the framework to deep representation learning, including representation hierarchy, latent-state estimation, and regularization-exploration trade-offs, and illustrate the framework through a case study on AI regulation debates.

</details>


### [254] [Missingness-MDPs: Bridging the Theory of Missing Data and POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12262)
*Joshua Wendland,Markel Zubia,Roman Andriushchenko,Maris F. L. Galesloot,Milan Ceska,Henrik von Kleist,Thiago D. Simao,Maximilian Weininger,Nils Jansen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出missingness-MDPs (miss-MDPs)，一种结合缺失数据理论的POMDP子类，通过利用缺失机制的结构属性设计PAC学习算法，从轨迹数据中学习观测函数，并证明所得策略在真实环境中是epsilon-最优的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准POMDP在观测数据存在缺失（如传感器故障、隐私保护等）时，其观测函数难以直接定义或学习；而现有方法无法保证在缺失机制未知的情况下得到接近最优的策略。

Method: 1) 定义miss-MDP，将观测函数建模为缺失函数；2) 区分三种缺失类型（MCAR, MAR, MNAR）；3) 利用各缺失类型的结构性质，设计PAC算法从轨迹数据中学习缺失函数；4) 将学到的缺失函数补全为完整的miss-MDP，并使用现成规划方法求解；5) 给出策略epsilon-最优性的理论证明。

Result: 理论证明了所提算法能以高概率得到epsilon-最优策略；实验结果表明该方法优于两种无模型POMDP方法。

Conclusion: 通过将缺失数据理论引入POMDP框架，miss-MDP有效解决了观测缺失下的规划问题，且利用缺失类型结构性质可实现可证的最优性保证，为实际应用中的缺失观测问题提供了理论支撑和实用方法。

Abstract: We introduce missingness-MDPs (miss-MDPs), a novel subclass of partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) that incorporates the theory of missing data. A miss-MDP is a POMDP whose observation function is a missingness function, specifying the probability that individual state features are missing (i.e., unobserved) at a time step. The literature distinguishes three canonical missingness types: missing (1) completely at random (MCAR), (2) at random (MAR), and (3) not at random (MNAR). Our planning problem is to compute near-optimal policies for a miss-MDP with an unknown missingness function, given a dataset of action-observation trajectories. Achieving such optimality guarantees for policies requires learning the missingness function from data, which is infeasible for general POMDPs. To overcome this challenge, we exploit the structural properties of different missingness types to derive probably approximately correct (PAC) algorithms for learning the missingness function. These algorithms yield an approximate but fully specified miss-MDP that we solve using off-the-shelf planning methods. We prove that, with high probability, the resulting policies are epsilon-optimal in the true miss-MDP. Empirical results confirm the theory and demonstrate superior performance of our approach over two model-free POMDP methods.

</details>


### [255] [How Useful Is Cross-Domain Generalization for Training LLM Monitors?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12265)
*Sam Martin,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了在多分类任务上微调语言模型是否能提升新领域的分类性能，发现其能部分泛化到邻近领域，但在提示完全改变而数据域不变时失败；通过混合通用指令跟随训练可缓解此问题，且无思维链的分类训练能泛化到需要思维链的分类和摘要任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究显示提示语言模型可在少样本分类中发挥作用，但缺乏微调的鲁棒性和性能优势。作者希望探究多任务分类训练是否能帮助模型更好地泛化到未见过的分类提示和新领域。

Method: 作者在多个分类任务上对语言模型进行微调（每个任务使用各自提示），然后在未见过的分类提示和新领域上测试泛化能力；进一步将分类训练与通用指令跟随训练混合，观察是否能缓解泛化失败。

Result: 多任务分类训练可部分泛化到邻近领域；但当分类提示完全改变而数据域不变时，微调模型无法正确跟随新提示；混合通用指令训练可保留分类训练优势并缓解泛化失败；无思维链分类训练能泛化到需要思维链的分类和摘要任务。

Conclusion: 无思维链的分类训练可能在构建其他分类器和监控系统方面具有工具性价值，值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Using prompted language models as classifiers enables classification in domains with limited training data, but misses some of the robustness and performance benefits that fine-tuning can bring. We study whether training on multiple classification tasks, each with its own prompt, improves performance on new domains with new classification prompts. We show that such training partially generalizes to adjacent domains, improving classification performance on tasks that are unseen during training. However, we identify specific edge cases where the fine-tuned models fail to follow prompts, such as when the classification prompt changes completely while the data domain remains the same as during training. We show that classification training can be mixed with general instruction following training, and that (when done well) such training keeps the benefits of classification training and mitigates its generalization failures. Surprisingly, we see that this no-thinking supervised classification training can generalize to with-thinking classification and summarization, suggesting that no-thinking classification training might be instrumentally useful in building other kinds of classifiers and monitoring systems.

</details>


### [256] [NARA: Anchor-Conditioned Relation-Aware Contextualization of Heterogeneous Geoentities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12276)
*Jina Kim,Gengchen Mai,Lingyi Zhao,Khurram Shafique,Yao-Yi Chiang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出NARA框架，一种面向矢量地理实体的自监督表示学习方法，通过联合建模语义、几何和空间关系（包括拓扑关系），在建筑功能分类、交通速度预测和POI推荐任务上优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矢量地理空间表示学习方法碎片化，通常局限于特定几何类型或仅建模邻近关系（如距离），未能统一捕获异质地理实体间的语义、几何与结构化空间关系（包括拓扑关系）。

Method: 提出NARA（Neural Anchor-conditioned Relation-Aware representation learning）框架，一种自监督学习框架，通过锚点条件机制联合建模语义、几何与空间关系（包括度量和拓扑关系），支持点、折线、多边形等异质地理实体的上下文感知表示学习。

Result: 在建筑功能分类、交通速度预测、下一个POI推荐三个任务上，NARA一致优于现有方法，证明统一关系建模对矢量地理空间数据的有效性。

Conclusion: 同时建模语义、几何与空间关系（超越简单邻近性）的联合表示学习能显著提升矢量地理实体的表征质量，NARA框架为异质矢量地理数据提供了统一的关系感知解决方案。

Abstract: Geospatial foundation models have primarily focused on raster data such as satellite imagery, where self-supervised learning has been widely studied. Vector geospatial data instead represent the world as discrete geoentities with explicit geometry, semantics, and structured spatial relations, including metric proximity and topological relationships. These relations jointly determine how entities interact within space, yet existing representation learning methods remain fragmented, often restricted to specific geometry types or partial spatial relations, limiting their ability to capture unified spatial context across heterogeneous geoentities. We propose NARA (Neural Anchor-conditioned Relation-Aware representation learning), a self-supervised framework for vector geoentities. NARA learns context-dependent representations by jointly modeling semantics, geometry, and spatial relations within a unified framework and captures relational spatial structure beyond proximity alone, enabling rich contextualized representations across heterogeneous geoentities of points, polylines, and polygons. Evaluation on building function classification, traffic speed prediction, and next point-of-interest recommendation shows consistent improvements over prior methods, highlighting the benefit of unified relational modeling for vector geospatial data.

</details>


### [257] [Executable Agentic Memory for GUI Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12294)
*Zerui Qin,Sheng Yue,Xingyuan Hua,Yongjian Fu,Ju Ren*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出可执行智能体记忆（EAM），一种结构化知识图谱方法，将GUI规划从自由生成转变为检索-执行流程，通过状态感知DFS、动作组挖掘和基于Q函数的蒙特卡洛树搜索，在AndroidWorld上比UI-TARS-7B提升19.6%，token成本降低6倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GUI智能体采用以模型为中心的逐步交互范式，LLM需要在每个屏幕重新理解UI并决策，在长程任务中表现脆弱，缺乏鲁棒性和效率。

Method: 构建可执行智能体记忆（EAM）知识图谱；使用状态感知DFS和动作组挖掘构建记忆；引入轻量级Q函数模型引导蒙特卡洛树搜索（MCTS）进行图搜索规划。

Result: 在AndroidWorld上比UI-TARS-7B提升19.6%；相比GPT-4o token成本降低6倍；平均延迟2.8秒。

Conclusion: EAM实现了可靠、快速的长程GUI自动化，理论层面建立了Q模型的偏置一致性并推导了路径恢复的样本复杂度界。

Abstract: Modern GUI agents typically rely on a model-centric and step-wise interaction paradigm, where LLMs must re-interpret the UI and re-decide actions at every screen, which is fragile in long-horizon tasks. In this paper, we propose Executable Agentic Memory (EAM), a structured Knowledge Graph (KG) that shifts GUI planning from free-form generation to a robust retrieval-and-execution process. Our approach includes a sample-efficient memory construction pipeline using state-aware DFS and action-group mining to compress multi-step routines. To ensure efficient planning, we introduce a value-guided graph search where a lightweight Q-function model steers Monte Carlo Tree Search (MCTS) over the KG. We theoretically establish bias-consistency for the Q-model and derive sample complexity bounds for path recovery. Empirically, EAM outperforms state-of-the-art baselines like UI-TARS-7B by up to $19.6\%$ on AndroidWorld, while reducing token costs $6\times$ relative to GPT-4o. With a $2.8$s average latency, EAM enables reliable, quick, and long-horizon GUI automation.

</details>


### [258] [LISA: Cognitive Arbitration for Signal-Free Autonomous Intersection Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12321)
*Abderrahmane Lakas,Mohamed Amine Ferrag,Merouane Debbah*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出LISA，一种基于LLM的无信号交叉口管理框架，通过推理车辆意图实现实时协调。相比传统方法，LISA在延误时间（降低89.1%）、排队长度（降低60.6%）、燃油消耗（降低48.8%）等方面均取得显著提升，证明了LLM作为主要决策者进行无信号交叉口管理的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有交叉口管理方法（如固定信号控制、SCATS、AIM等）在复杂动态交通场景下表现不佳，主要存在以下问题：信号控制器无法感知车辆优先级、基于预约的方法缺乏意图感知能力、现有LLM系统仍依赖信号基础设施。同时，LLM的推理延迟问题也制约了其在亚秒级控制场景中的应用。作者希望探索将LLM作为主要决策者，实现完全无信号的交叉口管理。

Method: 提出LISA（LLM-Based Intent-Driven Speed Advisory）框架：1）车辆声明意图（包括去向、优先级、能耗偏好等）；2）LLM作为认知仲裁器，推理车辆意图、优先级类别、队列压力和能耗偏好；3）生成速度建议，实时协调交叉口通行。

Result: 相比固定信号控制、SCATS、AIM和GLOSA等基线方法，LISA在多种交通负荷下表现优异：平均控制延误降低最多89.1%，在非LLM方法降级为F级时仍保持C级服务水平；近饱和状态下平均等待时间降低93%，峰值队列长度降低60.6%；燃油消耗降低最多48.8%；意图满足率达到86.2%（最佳非LLM方法为61.2%）。

Conclusion: LLM的推理能力可以支持实时、无信号的交叉口管理，LISA框架证明了LLM作为交叉口管理核心决策者的可行性，为智能交通系统中LLM的应用提供了新的方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) show strong potential for Intelligent Transportation Systems (ITS), particularly in tasks requiring situational reasoning and multi-agent coordination. These capabilities make them well suited for cooperative driving, where rule-based approaches struggle in complex and dynamic traffic environments. Intersection management remains especially challenging due to conflicting right-of-way demands, heterogeneous vehicle priorities, and vehicle-specific kinematic constraints that must be resolved in real time. However, existing approaches typically use LLMs as auxiliary components on top of signal-based systems rather than as primary decision-makers. Signal controllers remain vehicle-agnostic, reservation-based methods lack intent awareness, and recent LLM-based systems still depend on signal infrastructure. In addition, LLM inference latency limits their use in sub-second control settings.
  We propose LISA (LLM-Based Intent-Driven Speed Advisory), a signal-free cognitive arbitration framework for autonomous intersection management. LISA uses an LLM to reason over declared vehicle intents, incorporating priority classes, queue pressure, and energy preferences. We evaluate LISA against fixed-cycle control, SCATS, AIM, and GLOSA across varying traffic loads. Results show that LISA reduces mean control delay by up to 89.1% and maintains Level of Service C while all non-LLM baselines degrade to Level of Service F. Under near-saturated demand, LISA reduces mean waiting time by 93% and peak queue length by 60.6% relative to fixed-cycle control. It also lowers fuel consumption by up to 48.8% and achieves 86.2% intent satisfaction, compared to 61.2% for the best non-LLM method. These results demonstrate that LLM-based reasoning can enable real-time, signal-free intersection management.

</details>


### [259] [Towards Automated Air Traffic Safety Assessment Around Non-Towered Airports Using Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12332)
*Torsten Darrell,Mahyar Ghazanfari,Jordan Kam,Alexandre Bayen,Amin Tabrizian,Peng Wei*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出使用视觉-语言模型（VLM）分析非管制机场的飞行安全。通过处理CTAF无线电通信、METAR气象数据、ADS-B轨迹和目视航图等多模态数据，作者在半月湾机场进行了初步研究。定性案例验证了框架能识别路权违规行为；基于12类风险合成数据集的定量评估中，开源LLM在二分类任务上F1分数超过0.85。研究表明VLM分析非管制机场安全具有潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非管制机场依赖CTAF频道的飞行员自报通信协议，频繁发生空中接近事件（NMAC），亟需自动化安全分析方法来识别风险。

Method: 提出通用VLM框架，融合CTAF转录文本、METAR气象数据、ADS-B飞行轨迹和目视航图等多模态信息；在半月湾机场进行真实案例定性分析，并构建包含12类风险标签的合成数据集进行定量基准测试。

Result: 定性案例成功识别路权违规；在仅使用CTAF和METAR输入的条件下，开源LLM在二分类任务上宏F1>0.85；闭源模型（GPT-4o等）表现更优。

Conclusion: VLM分析非管制机场安全是可行的未来能力，但需进一步跨模态定量评估和更多真实数据验证。

Abstract: We investigate frameworks for post-flight safety analysis at non-towered airports using large language models (LLMs). Non-towered airports rely on the Common Traffic Advisory Frequency (CTAF) for air traffic coordination and experience frequent near mid-air collisions due to the pilot self-announcement communication protocol. We propose a general vision-language model (VLM) approach to analyze the transcribed CTAF radio communications in natural language, METeorological Aerodrome Report (METAR) weather data, Automatic Dependent Surveillance-Broadcast (ADS-B) flight trajectories, and Visual Flight Rules sectional charts of the airfield. We provide a preliminary study at Half Moon Bay Airport, with a qualitative real world case study and a quantitative evaluation using a new synthetic dataset of communications and weather modalities. We qualitatively evaluate our framework on real flight data using Gemini 2.5 Pro, demonstrating accurate identification of a right-of-way violation. The synthetic dataset is derived from real examples and includes a 12-category hazard taxonomy, and is used to benchmark three open-source (Qwen 2.5-7B, Mistral-7B, Gemma-2-9B) and three closed-source (GPT-4o, GPT-5.4, Claude Sonnet 4.6) LLM models on the subset of inputs related to CTAF and METAR. Even limited to CTAF and METAR inputs and open source LLMs, instances of our framework typically achieve a macro F1 score above 0.85 on a binary nominal/danger classification task. Future work includes a quantitative evaluation across all modalities and a larger number of real world examples. Taken together, our results suggest that VLM analysis of safety at non-towered airports may be a valuable future capability.

</details>


### [260] [Reinforcing VLAs in Task-Agnostic World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12334)
*Yucen Wang,Rui Yu,Fengming Zhang,Junjie Lu,Xinyao Qin,Tianxiang Zhang,Kaixin Wang,Li Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出RAW-Dream框架，通过任务无关的世界模型和VLM奖励生成，实现VLA模型的零样本策略微调，无需任务特定数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA后训练依赖任务特定数据来微调世界模型和奖励模型，限制了向未见任务的扩展能力。

Method: 1) 使用预训练在多样化任务无关行为上的世界模型预测未来轨迹；2) 利用现成VLM生成奖励；3) 引入双重噪声验证机制过滤不可靠的想象轨迹。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界实验中均取得一致的性能提升，证明了通用物理先验可以有效替代昂贵的任务依赖数据。

Conclusion: 任务无关的世界模型和VLM奖励生成能够实现VLA的高可扩展性适应，为机器人策略微调提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Post-training Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models via reinforcement learning (RL) in learned world models has emerged as an effective strategy to adapt to new tasks without costly real-world interactions. However, while using imagined trajectories reduces the sample complexity of policy training, existing methods still heavily rely on task-specific data to fine-tune both the world and reward models, fundamentally limiting their scalability to unseen tasks. To overcome this, we argue that world and reward models should capture transferable physical priors that enable zero-shot inference. We propose RAW-Dream (Reinforcing VLAs in task-Agnostic World Dreams), a new paradigm that completely disentangles world model learning from downstream task dependencies. RAW-Dream utilizes a world model pre-trained on diverse task-free behaviors for predicting future rollouts, and an off-the-shelf Vision-Language Model (VLM) for reward generation. Because both components are task-agnostic, VLAs can be readily finetuned for any new task entirely within this zero-shot imagination. Furthermore, to mitigate world model hallucinations, we introduce a dual-noise verification mechanism to filter out unreliable rollouts. Extensive experiments across simulation and real-world settings demonstrate consistent performance gains, proving that generalized physical priors can effectively substitute for costly task-dependent data, offering a highly scalable roadmap for VLA adaptation.

</details>


### [261] [$δ$-mem: Efficient Online Memory for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12357)
*Jingdi Lei,Di Zhang,Junxian Li,Weida Wang,Kaixuan Fan,Xiang Liu,Qihan Liu,Xiaoteng Ma,Baian Chen,Soujanya Poria*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了一种轻量级记忆机制δ-mem，通过紧凑的在线关联记忆状态（8×8矩阵）增强冻结的全注意力骨干网络，利用delta规则在线压缩历史信息并生成低秩校正信号，在不微调、不替换骨干网络、不扩展上下文窗口的条件下，在记忆密集型任务上取得显著提升（如MemoryAgentBench提升1.31倍）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在长期对话和智能体系统中需要积累和重用历史信息，但简单扩展上下文窗口成本高昂且难以保证有效利用，因此需要一种轻量、高效的记忆机制。

Method: 在冻结的全注意力骨干网络上附加一个紧凑的在线状态（关联记忆），通过delta规则学习将历史信息压缩为固定大小的状态矩阵，在生成时利用该矩阵的读出结果生成低秩校正，修改骨干网络的注意力计算。

Result: 仅用8×8的在线记忆状态，平均得分达到冻结骨干的1.10倍、最强非δ-mem基线方法的1.15倍；在MemoryAgentBench上达到1.31倍，在LoCoMo上达到1.20倍，同时基本保持通用能力。

Conclusion: 有效的记忆可以通过紧凑的在线状态直接耦合注意力计算来实现，无需全微调、替换骨干网络或显式扩展上下文窗口。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly need to accumulate and reuse historical information in long-term assistants and agent systems. Simply expanding the context window is costly and often fails to ensure effective context utilization. We propose $δ$-mem, a lightweight memory mechanism that augments a frozen full-attention backbone with a compact online state of associative memory. $δ$-mem compresses past information into a fixed-size state matrix updated by delta-rule learning, and uses its readout to generate low-rank corrections to the backbone's attention computation during generation. With only an $8\times8$ online memory state, $δ$-mem improves the average score to $1.10\times$ that of the frozen backbone and $1.15\times$ that of the strongest non-$δ$-mem memory baseline. It achieves larger gains on memory-heavy benchmarks, reaching $1.31\times$ on MemoryAgentBench and $1.20\times$ on LoCoMo, while largely preserving general capabilities. These results show that effective memory can be realized through a compact online state directly coupled with attention computation, without full fine-tuning, backbone replacement, or explicit context extension.

</details>


### [262] [Classifier Context Rot: Monitor Performance Degrades with Context Length](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12366)
*Sam Martin,Fabien Roger*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发现当前最先进的语言模型在监控超长编码代理对话（>500K tokens）时，对危险行为的漏检率随上下文长度增加而显著上升（最高达30倍），并提出了部分缓解方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Agent监控基准数据集上下文长度通常不超过100K tokens，而实际编码代理对话常超过500K tokens，导致对长上下文场景下模型监控能力的评估存在严重高估。

Method: 构建超长编码代理数据集（包含800K tokens以上良性活动后的危险行为），评估Opus 4.6、GPT 5.4、Gemini 3.1等前沿模型在不同上下文长度下对危险行为的检测率，并测试周期性提示等缓解策略。

Result: 所有评估模型在长上下文中漏检率显著升高：危险行为出现在800K tokens后时，漏检率是单独出现时的2倍到30倍；周期性提示可部分缓解该问题。

Conclusion: 现有监控评估方法因未考虑长上下文退化效应，高估了模型实际性能；未来需将长上下文评估纳入标准流程，并探索更好的后训练缓解策略。

Abstract: Monitoring coding agents for dangerous behavior using language models requires classifying transcripts that often exceed 500K tokens, but prior agent monitoring benchmarks rarely contain transcripts longer than 100K tokens. We show that when used as classifiers, current frontier models fail to notice dangerous actions more often in longer transcripts. In particular, on a dataset that requires identifying when a coding agent takes a subtly dangerous action, Opus 4.6, GPT 5.4, and Gemini 3.1 miss these actions $2\times$ to $30\times$ more often when they occur after 800K tokens of benign activity than when they occur on their own. We also show that these weaknesses can be partially mitigated with prompting techniques such as periodic reminders throughout the transcript and may be mitigated further with better post-training. Monitor evaluations that do not consider long-context degradation are likely overestimating monitor performance.

</details>


### [263] [ProfiliTable: Profiling-Driven Tabular Data Processing via Agentic Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12376)
*Wei Liu,Yang Gu,Xi Yan,Zihan Nan,Beicheng Xu,Keyao Ding,Bin Cui,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ProfiliTable是一个基于动态概要分析的自主多智能体框架，通过交互式探索、知识增强合成和反馈驱动优化，有效解决LLM在表格处理任务中因指令模糊、任务复杂和缺乏结构化反馈导致的代码语义错误问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM方法在自动化表格处理任务（清洗、转换、增强、匹配）时，常因指令模糊、任务结构复杂、缺乏结构化反馈而生成语法正确但语义错误的代码，亟需一种能够动态理解数据上下文并实现闭环优化的方法。

Method: 提出ProfiliTable多智能体框架，包含三个核心模块：(i) Profiler：采用ReAct风格进行数据探索以构建语义理解；(ii) Generator：检索经过筛选的操作符合成任务感知代码；(iii) Evaluator-Summarizer循环：注入执行分数和诊断洞察实现闭环优化。

Result: 在涵盖18种表格任务类型的多样基准测试上，ProfiliTable持续优于强基线方法，尤其在复杂多步场景中表现突出。

Conclusion: 动态概要分析对于将模糊的用户意图可靠地转化为健壮且符合治理规范的表格转换至关重要。

Abstract: Table processing-including cleaning, transformation, augmentation, and matching-is a foundational yet error-prone stage in real-world data pipelines. While recent LLM-based approaches show promise for automating such tasks, they often struggle in practice due to ambiguous instructions, complex task structures, and the lack of structured feedback, resulting in syntactically correct but semantically flawed code. To address these challenges, we propose ProfiliTable, an autonomous multi-agent framework centered on dynamic profiling, which constructs and iteratively refines a unified execution context through interactive exploration, knowledge-augmented synthesis, and feedback-driven refinement. ProfiliTable integrates (i) a Profiler that performs ReAct-style data exploration to build semantic understanding, (ii) a Generator that retrieves curated operators to synthesize task-aware code, and (iii) an Evaluator-Summarizer loop that injects execution scores and diagnostic insights to enable closed-loop refinement. Extensive experiments on a diverse benchmark covering 18 tabular task types demonstrate that ProfiliTable consistently outperforms strong baselines, particularly in complex multi-step scenarios. These results highlight the critical role of dynamic profiling in reliably translating ambiguous user intents into robust and governance-compliant table transformations.

</details>


### [264] [Semantic Reward Collapse and the Preservation of Epistemic Integrity in Adaptive AI Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12406)
*William Parris*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"语义奖励崩溃"(SRC)概念，指出在基于人类反馈的强化学习(RLHF)中，不同性质的评估不满被压缩为统一的优化信号，导致模型出现确定性表演、幻觉式连贯、校准漂移、谄媚行为等问题。作者提出应将不确定性披露视为受保护的认知行为，并引入"宪法性奖励分层"(CRS)框架作为解决方案方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLHF和偏好优化虽提升了LLM的可用性，但存在确定性表演、幻觉连贯性、校准漂移、谄媚行为等结构性缺陷，表明标量化的偏好优化系统存在未解决的结构性问题。

Method: 提出语义奖励崩溃(SRC)理论：不同认知类别(事实错误、不确定性披露、格式不满、延迟等)的评估不满被压缩到共享的奖励拓扑中，导致优化信号语义混淆。据此提出宪法性奖励分层(CRS)框架作为治理导向的解决方案。

Result: 论文为概念性/理论性工作，尚未提供实证验证结果。CRS被明确定位为"可测试的治理导向研究方向"，需进一步实证研究。

Conclusion: 不确定性披露和升级行为应被视为受保护的认知行为，而非全局惩罚的任务未完成。CRS框架旨在在自适应学习系统中保持差异化的认知归属，但目前仅为理论构想。

Abstract: Recent advances in reinforcement learning from human feedback (RLHF) and preference optimization have substantially improved the usability, coherence, and safety of large language models. However, recurring behaviors such as performative certainty, hallucinated continuity, calibration drift, sycophancy, and suppression of visible uncertainty suggest unresolved structural issues within scalarized preference optimization systems.
  We propose Semantic Reward Collapse (SRC): the compression of semantically distinct forms of evaluative dissatisfaction into generalized optimization signals. Under SRC, categories such as factual incorrectness, uncertainty disclosure, formatting dissatisfaction, latency, and social preference may become entangled within a shared reward topology despite representing fundamentally different epistemic classes.
  We argue that adaptive reasoning systems operating under generalized evaluative pressure may drift toward suppression of visible epistemic failure rather than preservation of calibrated uncertainty integrity. These behaviors are framed strictly as optimization consequences rather than evidence of deception or anthropomorphic agency.
  Drawing on institutional proxy collapse, metric gaming, software reliability engineering, and human learning theory, we propose that uncertainty disclosure and escalation behavior should be treated as protected epistemic conduct rather than globally penalized task incompletion.
  Finally, we introduce Constitutional Reward Stratification (CRS), a domain-aware reward framework intended to preserve differentiated epistemic attribution within adaptive learning systems. We present CRS not as a validated solution, but as a testable governance-oriented research direction requiring further empirical investigation.

</details>


### [265] [Formalize, Don't Optimize: The Heuristic Trap in LLM-Generated Combinatorial Solvers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12421)
*Haoyu Wang,Yuliang Song,Tao Li,Zhiwei Deng,Yaqing Wang,Deepak Ramachandran,Eldan Cohen,Dan Roth*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出CP-SynC-XL基准测试（100个组合优化问题），比较三种求解器构建范式：原生Python、Python+OR-Tools、MiniZinc+OR-Tools。研究发现Python+OR-Tools正确率最高，而提示模型优化搜索仅带来微小加速（1.03-1.12x）且常导致正确率下降。建议LLM应仅用于形式化变量/约束/目标，搜索优化需单独验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在复杂组合问题上直接推理能力不足，近期神经符号系统开始用LLM合成可执行求解器。但求解器的表示方式（Python算法、约束建模API、声明式建模）以及是否应让LLM优化搜索策略，仍是未解决的核心设计问题。

Method: 构建CP-SynC-XL基准（100个组合问题，4577个实例），对比三种范式：原生Python算法搜索、Python+OR-Tools（约束建模API）、MiniZinc+OR-Tools（声明式约束建模）。评估正确率、覆盖率和搜索优化效果，并进行代码级审计。

Result: Python+OR-Tools正确率最高；MiniZinc+OR-Tools覆盖度最低；原生Python易返回格式正确但验证失败的结果。搜索优化提示仅带来1.03-1.12x中位数加速，呈双峰分布（许多实例变慢，长尾问题正确率骤降）。代码审计发现三种范式下各自特定的启发式陷阱。

Conclusion: 建议保守设计原则：LLM应仅用于形式化变量、约束和目标（交由验证求解器处理），任何LLM编写的搜索优化策略需单独验证后再使用。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) struggle to solve complex combinatorial problems through direct reasoning, so recent neuro-symbolic systems increasingly use them to synthesize executable solvers. A central design question is how the LLM should represent the solver, and whether it should also attempt to optimize search. We introduce CP-SynC-XL, a benchmark of 100 combinatorial problems (4,577 instances), and evaluate three solver-construction paradigms: native algorithmic search (Python), constraint modeling through a Python solver API (Python + OR-Tools), and declarative constraint modeling (MiniZinc + OR-Tools). We find a consistent representational divergence: Python + OR-Tools attains the highest correctness across LLMs, while MiniZinc + OR-Tools has lower absolute coverage despite using the same OR-Tools back-end. Native Python is the most likely to return a schema-valid solution that fails verification, whereas solver-backed paths preserve higher conditional fidelity. On the heuristic axis, prompting for search optimization yields only small median speed-ups (1.03-1.12x) and a strongly bimodal effect: many instances slow down, and correctness drops sharply on a long tail of problems. A paired code-level audit traces these regressions to a recurring heuristic trap. Under an efficiency-oriented prompt, the LLM may replace complete search with local approximations (Python), inject unverified bounds (Python + OR-Tools), or add redundant declarative machinery that overwhelms or over-constrains the model (MiniZinc + OR-Tools). These findings support a conservative design principle for LLM-generated combinatorial solvers: use the LLM primarily to formalize variables, constraints, and objectives for verified solvers, and separately check any LLM-authored search optimization before use.

</details>


### [266] [CAAFC: Chronological Actionable Automated Fact-Checker for misinformation / non-factual hallucination detection and correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12436)
*Islam Eldifrawi,Shengrui Wang,Amine Trabelsi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: CAAFC是一个超越现有SOTA的自动化事实核查框架，不仅能检测和纠正事实错误与幻觉，还能动态更新证据库，支持对声明、对话等多种文本类型的可操作验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AFC系统与专业事实核查员的实际操作存在严重脱节，且无法应对AI生成内容中的幻觉问题，手动核查海量内容已不可行。

Method: CAAFC框架通过对声明、对话和聊天记录进行操作，结合主要信息源提供可操作的纠正依据，并能动态整合最新上下文信息更新证据与知识库。

Result: 在多个基准数据集上超越当前最先进的AFC和幻觉检测系统。

Conclusion: CAAFC通过弥合现有系统与实际核查需求之间的差距，提升了事实验证的可靠性。

Abstract: With the vast amount of content uploaded every hour, along with the AI generated content that can include hallucinations, Automated Fact-Checking (AFC) has become increasingly vital, as it is infeasible for human fact-checkers to manually verify the sheer volume of information generated online. Professional fact-checkers have identified several gaps in existing AFC systems, noting a misalignment between how these systems operate and how fact-checking is performed in practice. In this paper, we introduce CAAFC (Chronological Actionable Automated Fact-Checker), a frame-work designed to bridge these gaps. It surpasses SOTA AFC and hallucination detection systems across multiple benchmark datasets. CAAFC operates on claims, conversations, and dialogues, enabling it not only to detect factual errors and hallucinations, but also to correct them by providing actionable justifications supported by primary information sources. Furthermore, CAAFC can update evidence and knowledge bases by incorporating recent and contextual information when necessary, thereby enhancing the reliability of fact verification.

</details>


### [267] [Towards Affordable Energy: A Gymnasium Environment for Electric Utility Demand-Response Programs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12462)
*Jose E. Aguilar Escamilla,Lingdong Zhou,Xiangqi Zhu,Huazheng Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DR-Gym，一个面向电力公司视角的开源在线强化学习环境，用于训练和评估需求响应项目，解决离线数据无法捕捉动态交互反馈循环的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 极端天气和波动的电力批发市场使居民面临财务风险，但配电网侧的需求响应作为提升电网灵活性和能源可负担性的工具仍未得到充分利用。

Method: 构建DR-Gym在线Gymnasium兼容环境，包含：基于实际极端事件校准的体制切换批发电价模型、基于物理的建筑需求曲线、以及可配置的多目标奖励函数。

Result: 通过基线策略和数据快照验证了该环境能够创建真实且可学习的环境。

Conclusion: DR-Gym为解决离线数据无法捕捉动态交互反馈的难题提供了有效平台，推动了强化学习在需求响应优化中的应用。

Abstract: Extreme weather and volatile wholesale electricity markets expose residential consumers to catastrophic financial risks, yet demand response at the distribution level remains an underutilized tool for grid flexibility and energy affordability. While a demand-response program can shield consumers by issuing financial credits during high-price periods, optimizing this sequential decision-making process presents a unique challenge for reinforcement learning despite the plentiful offline historical smart meter and wholesale pricing data available publicly. Offline historical data fails to capture the dynamic, interactive feedback loop between an electric utility's pricing signals and customer acceptance and adaptation to a demand-response program. To address this, we introduce DR-Gym, an open-source, online Gymnasium-compatible environment designed to train and evaluate demand-response from the electric utility's perspective. Unlike existing device-level energy simulators, our environment focuses on the market-level electric utility setting and provides a rich observational space relevant to the electric utility. The simulator additionally features a regime-switching wholesale price model calibrated to real-world extreme events, alongside physics-based building demand profiles. For our learning signal, we use a configurable, multi-objective reward function for specifying diverse learning objectives. We demonstrate through baseline strategies and data snapshots the capability of our simulator to create realistic and learnable environments.

</details>


### [268] [Reward Hacking in Rubric-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12474)
*Anas Mahmoud,MohammadHossein Rezaei,Zihao Wang,Anisha Gunjal,Bing Liu,Yunzhong He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了基于评估标准（rubric）的强化学习中的奖励破解问题，发现弱验证器会导致奖励虚高无法迁移，强验证器能减少但无法完全消除该问题，并提出了一个无验证器的诊断指标——自我内化差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在基于rubric的强化学习中，奖励破解会导致策略在训练验证器上得分高但在其他评估者上表现差，需要系统性地分析这一问题的来源及缓解方法。

Method: 提出一个框架将奖励破解来源分为两类：验证器失败（训练验证器错误地给分）和rubric设计缺陷（rubric本身无法捕捉质量）。引入自我内化差距作为无验证器诊断指标，在医学和科学领域进行实验。

Result: 弱验证器产生无法迁移的奖励增益，且随训练加剧；强验证器能减少但不能消除奖励破解；自我内化差距能有效跟踪验证器质量；更强的验证无法在rubric遗漏关键失败模式时防止奖励破解。

Conclusion: 更强的验证能减少但无法完全消除奖励破解，rubric增益未必对应更广泛的质量提升，需要超越单一验证器强度设计更鲁棒的评估机制。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards has enabled strong post-training gains in domains such as math and coding, though many open-ended settings rely on rubric-based rewards. We study reward hacking in rubric-based RL, where a policy is optimized against a training verifier but evaluated against a cross-family panel of three frontier judges, reducing dependence on any single evaluator. Our framework separates two sources of divergence: verifier failure, where the training verifier credits rubric criteria that reference verifiers reject, and rubric-design limitations, where even strong rubric-based verifiers favor responses that rubric-free judges rate worse overall. Across medical and science domains, weak verifiers produce large proxy-reward gains that do not transfer to the reference verifiers; exploitation grows over training and concentrates in recurring failures such as partial satisfaction of compound criteria, treating implicit content as explicit, and imprecise topical matching. Stronger verifiers substantially reduce, but do not eliminate, verifier exploitation. We also introduce a self-internalization gap, a verifier-free diagnostic based on policy log-probabilities, which tracks reference-verifier quality, detecting when the policy trained using the weak verifier stops improving. Finally, in our setting, stronger verification does not prevent reward hacking when the rubric leaves important failure modes unspecified: rubric-based verifiers prefer the RL checkpoint, while rubric-free judges prefer the base model. These disagreements coincide with gains concentrated in completeness and presence-based criteria, alongside declines in factual correctness, conciseness, relevance, and overall quality. Together, these results suggest that stronger verification reduces reward hacking, but does not by itself ensure that rubric gains correspond to broader quality gains.

</details>


### [269] [ToolCUA: Towards Optimal GUI-Tool Path Orchestration for Computer Use Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.12481)
*Xuhao Hu,Xi Zhang,Haiyang Xu,Kyle Qiao,Jingyi Yang,Xuanjing Huang,Jing Shao,Ming Yan,Jieping Ye*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ToolCUA是一个端到端智能体，通过分阶段训练范式学习最优的GUI-工具路径选择，在OSWorld-MCP上达到46.85%的准确率，相对基线提升约66%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CUA在混合动作空间（GUI原子操作+工具调用）中难以决定何时使用GUI、何时切换工具，原因是缺乏高质量的交错GUI-工具轨迹、真实轨迹收集成本高且脆弱、缺乏轨迹级别的路径选择监督。

Method: 1) Interleaved GUI-Tool轨迹扩展管线：复用静态GUI轨迹并合成工具库，生成多样化交错轨迹；2) Tool-Bootstrapped GUI RFT：结合SFT预热和单轮强化学习优化切换点决策；3) Online Agentic RL：在高保真GUI-工具环境中，使用工具高效路径奖励进行在线强化学习优化。

Result: OSWorld-MCP上准确率46.85%，相对基线提升约66%，比纯GUI设置提升3.9%，达到同类模型规模SOTA。

Conclusion: 在混合动作空间中训练是实现真实世界数字智能体的有前景范式。

Abstract: Computer Use Agents (CUAs) can act through both atomic GUI actions, such as click and type, and high-level tool calls, such as API-based file operations, but this hybrid action space often leaves them uncertain about when to continue with GUI actions or switch to tools, leading to suboptimal execution paths. This difficulty stems from the scarcity of high-quality interleaved GUI-Tool trajectories, the cost and brittleness of collecting real tool trajectories, and the lack of trajectory-level supervision for GUI-Tool path selection. In this paper, we propose ToolCUA, an end-to-end agent designed to learn optimal GUI-Tool path selection through a staged training paradigm. We first introduce an Interleaved GUI-Tool Trajectory Scaling Pipeline that repurposes abundant static GUI trajectories and synthesizes a grounded tool library, enabling diverse GUI-Tool trajectories without manual engineering or real tool-trajectory collection. We then perform Tool-Bootstrapped GUI RFT, combining warmup SFT with single-turn RL to improve decisions at critical GUI-Tool switching points. Finally, we optimize ToolCUA with Online Agentic RL in a high-fidelity GUI-Tool environment, guided by a Tool-Efficient Path Reward that encourages appropriate tool use and shorter execution paths. Experiments on OSWorld-MCP show that ToolCUA achieves 46.85% accuracy, a relative improvement of approximately 66% over the baseline, establishing a new state of the art among models of comparable scale. It also improves by 3.9% over GUI-only settings, demonstrating effective GUI-Tool orchestration. The results further suggest that training in a hybrid action space is a promising paradigm for real-world digital agents. Open-sourced here: https://x-plug.github.io/ToolCUA/

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [270] [ECHO: Continuous Hierarchical Memory for Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10993)
*Yanbin Hu,Jin Cui,Jiayi Lu,Ruixuan Yang,Jun Ye,Boran Zhao,Xingyu Chen,Xuguang Lan,Pengju Ren*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ECHO框架，通过双曲自编码器将VLA模型隐状态映射到连续层次空间，构建语义记忆树，实现高效检索与组合泛化，在LIBERO-Long上提升12.8%绝对成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆增强VLA模型采用线性或扁平存储方式，缺乏操作类别的结构先验与层次组织，限制了长程任务中的经验检索效率与对未见组合任务的泛化能力。

Method: 1) 双曲自编码器将VLA隐状态映射到连续层次空间；2) 利用双曲度量与包含约束机制构建语义记忆树，支持自上而下检索；3) 背景巩固机制通过几何插值与结构分裂不断优化记忆树，支持虚拟记忆合成。

Result: 集成到π₀基础模型中，在LIBERO-Long上执行成功率绝对提升12.8%，在跨套件未见长程任务上组合泛化能力也得到提升。

Conclusion: 受人类经验层次组织启发，将VLA隐状态组织为连续层次空间中的语义记忆树，有效提升了长程操作任务的执行成功率与组合泛化能力。

Abstract: Memory capacity is a critical factor determining the performance of Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models in long-horizon manipulation tasks. Existing memory-augmented architectures primarily rely on linear or flat storage, lacking structural priors for manipulation categories and hierarchical organization. This deficiency hinders efficient experience retrieval and limits generalization to unseen long-horizon task compositions. Inspired by the hierarchical organization of human experience, we propose ECHO (Experience Consolidation and Hierarchical Organization), a novel memory framework operating within a Continuous Hierarchical Space. By employing a hyperbolic autoencoder, ECHO maps VLA hidden states into this space. Leveraging hyperbolic metrics and entailment constraint mechanisms, experience vectors are organized into a semantic memory tree that supports efficient top-down retrieval. In parallel, a background consolidation mechanism continuously refines the memory tree through geometric interpolation and structural splitting, supporting virtual memory synthesis in the continuous space. We integrate ECHO into the $π_0$ foundation model. Evaluations on LIBERO and preliminary real-world experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our approach, notably achieving a 12.8% absolute improvement in execution success rate over the $π_0$ baseline on LIBERO-Long, while improving compositional generalization on cross-suite unseen long-horizon tasks.

</details>


### [271] [ForceFlow: Learning to Feel and Act via Contact-Driven Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11048)
*Shuoheng Zhang,Yifu Yuan,Hongyao Tang,Yan Zheng,Qiaojun Yu,Pengyi Li,Guowei Huang,Helong Huang,Xingyue Quan,Jianye Hao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: ForceFlow是一个基于流匹配的力感知反应式框架，采用非对称多模态融合架构将力作为全局调控信号，通过视觉-触觉两阶段分层分解和V2F交接机制，在六类真实接触密集型任务中相比强基线ForceVLA提升37%的成功率，并展现出优异的力自调节与零样本泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有模仿学习方法在接触密集型操作任务中表现不佳，主要由于复杂接触动力学需要高精度力反馈与控制，而目前将力/力矩传感融入策略的方法尚未建立简单有效的框架来实现多模态观测下的鲁棒泛化。

Method: ForceFlow包含两个核心设计：1）接触阶段策略采用非对称多模态融合架构，将力信号作为全局调控信号，结合联合预测范式实现力与运动的深度耦合；2）任务级分层分解将操作分为视觉主导的接近阶段（基于VLM的指向定位）和触觉主导的交互阶段（力驱动接触执行），通过Vision-to-Force (V2F) 交接机制显式解耦空间泛化与接触调控。

Result: 在六类真实接触密集型任务上，ForceFlow相比强基线ForceVLA提升37%的成功率，同时保持显著更低的成本；在力信号预测、接触力自调节以及零样本分布外泛化方面均展现出优越性能。

Conclusion: ForceFlow通过非对称多模态融合与视觉-触觉分层分解，有效解决了接触密集型操作中的力-运动深度耦合问题，为机器人操作提供了一种兼具高性能与低成本优势的实用框架。

Abstract: Existing imitation learning methods enable robots to interact autonomously with the physical environment. However, contact-rich manipulation tasks remain a significant challenge due to complex contact dynamics that demand high-precision force feedback and control. Although recent efforts have attempted to integrate force/torque sensing into policies, how to build a simple yet effective framework that achieves robust generalization under multimodal observations remains an open question. In this paper, we propose ForceFlow, a force-aware reactive framework built upon flow matching. For contact-stage policy design, we investigate force signal fusion mechanisms and adopt an asymmetric multimodal fusion architecture that treats force as a global regulatory signal, combined with a joint prediction paradigm that enhances the policy's understanding of instantaneous force and historical information, thereby achieving deep coupling between force and motion. For task-level hierarchical decomposition, we divide manipulation into a vision-dominant approach stage (VLM-based pointing for target localization) and a touch-dominant interaction stage (force-driven contact execution), with a Vision-to-Force (V2F) handover mechanism that explicitly decouples spatial generalization from contact regulation. Experimental results across six real-world contact-rich tasks demonstrate that ForceFlow achieves a 37% success rate improvement over the strong baseline ForceVLA while maintaining significantly lower cost. Moreover, ForceFlow exhibits accurate force signal prediction and demonstrates superior performance in contact force self-regulation and zero-shot out-of-distribution (OOD) generalization.

</details>


### [272] [SEVO: Semantic-Enhanced Virtual Observation for Robust VLA Manipulation via Active Illumination and Data-Centric Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11114)
*Tianchonghui Fang,Yuan Zhuang,Fei Miao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SEVO是一种数据驱动方法，通过改进观测设计（体固相机、红色照明、YOLO语义覆盖）和多样化数据采集，使低成本VLA策略在跨环境操作中成功率从30-35%提升至75-85%，无需修改模型架构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA和模仿学习策略在训练环境外部署时频繁失败，社区报告显示迁移到新环境后成功率近乎为零，而现有基准测试仅在受控固定背景下评估，存在严重泛化鸿沟。

Method: SEVO方法包含：(1)体固相机覆盖完整操作空间；(2)红色光谱主动照明物理归一化物体外观；(3)实时YOLO分割提供背景不变语义线索。关键是采用多样化数据采集协议（系统变化光照、背景和干扰物）。

Result: 训练环境：ACT 95%、SmolVLA 83% grasp success；新环境迁移：ACT 85%、SmolVLA 75%。无SEVO时训练仅75%/70%，新环境骤降至30-35%。

Conclusion: 原则性的观测设计和数据采集多样性（而非模型规模扩展）是低成本机器人在日常家庭环境中可靠运行的关键因素。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) and imitation-learning policies trained via community toolchains on low-cost hardware frequently fail when deployed outside the training environment. Existing evaluations, including the original ACT and SmolVLA benchmarks, demonstrate high success rates under controlled, fixed backgrounds, yet community practitioners report near-zero transfer to new environments. We present SEVO (Semantic-Enhanced Virtual Observation), a data-centric approach that improves cross-environment manipulation robustness without modifying the policy architecture. SEVO transforms the raw RGB camera stream through three mechanisms: (1) body-fixed cameras whose combined fields of view cover the full manipulation workspace, (2) active red-spectrum illumination that physically normalizes object appearance, and (3) real-time YOLO segmentation overlay that provides a background-invariant semantic cue. Critically, we show that a diversified data collection protocol (systematically varying lighting, backgrounds, and distractors during teleoperation) is the single most important factor for generalization. We target transparent water bottles, objects that visually blend with their surroundings, and select a simple pick-and-place task to enable hundreds of controlled real-robot trials across two mobile platforms. The full pipeline achieves 95% grasp success with ACT and 83% with SmolVLA in the training environment, transferring to novel environments at 85% and 75%. Without SEVO, the same policies achieve only 75%/70% in training and collapse to 30-35% in novel environments. Our results demonstrate that principled observation design and environmental diversity during data collection, not model scaling, enable low-cost robots to operate reliably in everyday household environments.

</details>


### [273] [ASIP-Planner: Adaptive Planning for UAV Surface Inspection in Partially Known Indoor Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11119)
*Hanyu Jin,Zhefan Xu,Haoyu Shen,Xinming Han,Kanlong Ye,Kenji Shimada*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种自适应无人机检测框架，结合基于分段的全局覆盖规划器与面向检测的局部视角调整模块，在部分已知的结构化室内环境中实现高效、自适应的表面覆盖检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内基础设施检测（如隧道、工业设施）中，无人机依赖基于平面图的参考地图进行检测，但实际环境中可能存在未预见的障碍物（如临时结构或设备），导致视角遮挡、检测质量下降。现有覆盖规划方法假设环境完全已知，无法应对执行时的环境偏差。

Method: 提出自适应无人机检测框架，包含：(1) 基于分段的全局覆盖规划器，将平面检测目标组织为表面对齐的簇，生成紧凑且朝向一致的视角序列；(2) 面向检测的局部视角调整模块，在线调整观察方向以缓解遮挡引起的覆盖损失，同时保持轨迹结构并生成无碰撞轨迹。

Result: 在随机场景配置的仿真中，所提出的全局规划器实现了接近完全的覆盖，同时相比基线方法缩短了轨迹长度。真实飞行实验进一步验证了该框架能生成可用于下游分析的有效检测数据。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架在部分已知的结构化室内环境中提升了检测效率和适应性。

Abstract: Indoor infrastructure inspection, such as tunnels and industrial facilities, requires systematic surface coverage to ensure that all inspection targets are properly observed. Unmanned Aerial Vehicles (UAVs) offer an alternative to manual inspection by conducting map-guided surface inspection using prior structural models. However, in practice, indoor inspection often relies on floorplan-derived reference maps that may not reflect unforeseen obstacles, such as temporary structures or equipment, leading to occluded viewpoints and degraded inspection quality. Existing coverage planning methods typically assume a fully known inspection environment and perform deterministic global viewpoint optimization based on accurate prior maps, making them vulnerable to environmental discrepancies during execution. This work presents an adaptive UAV inspection framework for partially known structured indoor environments. The proposed method integrates a segment-based global coverage planner with an inspection-oriented local view-angle adaptation module. The global planner organizes planar inspection targets into surface-aligned clusters to generate compact viewpoint sequences with improved orientation consistency. The local planner generates collision-free trajectories and adjusts the viewing direction online to mitigate occlusion-induced coverage loss while preserving the planned trajectory structure. The simulation results across randomized scene configurations demonstrate that the proposed global planner achieves near-complete coverage while reducing trajectory length compared to representative baselines. Real-world flight experiments further validate that the framework produces usable inspection data for downstream analysis. These results indicate that the proposed framework improves inspection efficiency and adaptability in partially known structured indoor environments.

</details>


### [274] [Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting for Predictive 3D Representation in Language-Guided Pick-and-Place Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11144)
*Kaixin Jia,Jiacheng Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: Forecast-GS是一种用于语言条件机器人操作的预测性3D表示框架，通过显式建模任务完成状态，在真实世界的拾放任务中优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作系统通常只对当前场景进行推理，没有显式建模任务完成后的状态，这在部分观测条件下评估候选动作是否导致可行且符合任务目标的结果时存在局限。

Method: 提出Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting (Forecast-GS)框架，利用3D高斯泼溅预测任务完成状态，结合语言理解、3D感知和操作规划，实现语言条件下的机器人操作。

Result: 在Cutter-to-Box、Apple-to-Bowl、Sponge-to-Tray三个真实世界任务中，分别达到21/25、23/25、16/25的成功率，优于ReKep基线的15/25、19/25、10/25。人工辅助设置进一步提升至23/25、24/25、19/25。

Conclusion: 显式预测任务完成的3D状态能够实现更可靠的动作评估，但自动排序的不完善仍是完全自主操作的重要挑战。Forecast-GS为语言理解、3D感知与机器人操作规划之间提供了可解释的桥梁。

Abstract: We introduce Forecast-aware Gaussian Splatting (Forecast-GS), a predictive 3D representation framework for language-conditioned robotic manipulation. While recent manipulation systems have made progress by grounding language instructions into robot affordances, value maps, or relational keypoint constraints, they usually reason over the current scene and do not explicitly model the task-completed state. This limitation is critical when success depends on satisfying spatial and semantic goals under partial observations, where the robot must evaluate whether a candidate action leads to a feasible task-consistent outcome.
  We validate Forecast-GS on real-world pick-and-place manipulation tasks, including Cutter-to-Box, Apple-to-Bowl, and Sponge-to-Tray. For each task, we conduct 25 real-world trials under varied initial object configurations using the same robot platform and sensing setup. Forecast-GS with automatic candidate selection achieves success rates of 21/25, 23/25, and 16/25 on the three tasks, respectively, outperforming the ReKep baseline, which achieves 15/25, 19/25, and 10/25. A diagnostic human-assisted setting further improves success rates to 23/25, 24/25, and 19/25, suggesting that candidate generation is effective while automatic ranking remains imperfect. These results suggest that explicitly forecasting task-completed 3D states enables more reliable action evaluation, while the gap between automatic and human-assisted selection indicates that robust final-state ranking remains an important challenge for fully autonomous manipulation. Overall, Forecast-GS provides an interpretable bridge between language understanding, 3D perception, and robotic manipulation planning.

</details>


### [275] [Distributed Pose Graph Optimization via Continuous Riemannian Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11210)
*Jaeho Shin,Maani Ghaffari,Yulun Tian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于李群上二阶连续时间动力系统的分布式位姿图优化(PGO)框架，将位姿变量建模为受阻尼的质点，利用阻尼欧拉-庞加莱方程和半隐式几何积分器设计优化算法，在分布式多机器人场景下实现高效同步与异步优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分布式PGO方法在通信延迟、收敛速度和并行效率方面存在不足，需要一种理论完备且实用高效的分布式优化框架。

Method: 将PGO建模为李群上的二阶连续时间动力系统（阻尼欧拉-庞加莱方程），采用半隐式几何积分器进行离散化；设计块对角质量矩阵和阻尼矩阵实现分布式并行；利用状态和速度建模实现邻居预测以应对通信延迟。

Result: 在基准PGO数据集上，所提方法在同步和异步分布式设置下均优于现有最先进的分布式基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架统一了黎曼梯度下降和高斯-牛顿等算法，理论保证了能量耗散，实验验证了优越性能。

Abstract: We present a framework for distributed Pose Graph Optimization (PGO) by formulating the problem as a second-order continuous-time dynamical system evolving on Lie groups. By modeling pose variables as massive particles subject to damping, the equilibrium points of the resulting Riemannian dynamics coincide with first-order critical points of the original PGO problem. Using the governing damped Euler--Poincaré equations and a semi-implicit geometric integrator, we design an optimization algorithm that generalizes existing algorithms such as Riemannian gradient descent and Gauss--Newton. In multi-robot settings, we present a fully distributed and parallel method based on block-diagonal mass and damping matrices, where each robot solves an ordinary differential equation for its own poses with minimal communication overhead. Moreover, modeling both state and velocity enables principled neighbor prediction that significantly improves convergence under delayed communication. Theoretically, we present an analysis and establish sufficient condition that ensures energy dissipation under the employed geometric discretization scheme. Experiments on benchmark PGO datasets demonstrate that the proposed solver achieves superior performance compared to state-of-the-art distributed baselines in both synchronous and asynchronous regimes.

</details>


### [276] [Computational Design of a Low-Visibility UAV Using a Human-Aligned Perceptual Metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11296)
*Jingxian Wang,Chen Yu,David Matthews,Emma Alexander,Sam Kriegman,Michael Rubenstein*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为Phantom Twist的单桨无人机，通过高速旋转和运动模糊实现低可视度，并开发了两阶段自动化设计流程来优化组件布局，验证了该设计的稳定性和低感知性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多旋翼无人机（如四轴飞行器）在视觉上显著，容易被察觉。作者希望通过高速旋转产生的运动模糊来降低无人机的视觉感知度，从而提高隐蔽性。

Method: 设计了一种单桨无人机Phantom Twist，利用高速旋转和运动模糊降低可见性。开发了两阶段自动化设计流程，优化电池、控制PCB、电机-螺旋桨组件和配重的位置，在满足惯性约束和气动约束的前提下，最小化人类感知指标LPIPS。

Result: 通过制造和飞行测试多个原型，验证了设计流程能产生稳定可控的无人机，优化后的无人机相比传统四轴飞行器视觉感知度显著降低。

Conclusion: Phantom Twist通过高速旋转实现低可视度是可行的，自动化设计流程能有效平衡隐身性能与飞行稳定性，为未来低感知无人机设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: We introduce Phantom Twist, a type of single-propeller UAV designed to achieve low visibility through high-speed spinning and the exploitation of motion blur. We develop a two-stage automated design pipeline that optimizes the placement of functional components including batteries, control PCB, motor-propeller assembly, and counterweights. The pipeline minimizes visibility as measured by a human-aligned perceptual metric (LPIPS) while strictly satisfying inertial and aerodynamic constraints required for stable flight. We validate this approach through fabrication and flight testing of multiple prototypes. These tests confirm that our pipeline produces stable, controllable designs and that the optimized UAV exhibits significantly reduced visual perceptibility compared to conventional quadcopters.

</details>


### [277] [Kairos: A Scalable Serving System for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11381)
*Yinwei Dai,Ganesh Ananthanarayanan,Landon Cox,Xenofon Foukas,Bozidar Radunovic,Ravi Netravali*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Kairos，首个面向多机器人物理AI的智能服务系统，通过将生成-执行循环作为一等公民并主动参与执行阶段，相比数字AI服务系统降低了31.8-66.5%的端到端任务延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 物理AI任务的推理特性（多轮推理-执行交错、批量动作生成、异步执行）与数字AI截然不同，现有数字AI服务系统无法适配物理AI的需求，亟需专门设计。

Method: 提出Kairos系统，将"生成-执行"循环作为一等公民，让其主动参与执行阶段。通过深度耦合推理与执行过程，优化多机器人场景下的推理调度与执行协同。

Result: 在多种物理AI模型和机器人平台上，相比最先进的数字AI服务方案，平均端到端任务延迟降低31.8%-66.5%，且性能增益随机器人集群规模扩大而提升。

Conclusion: 物理AI需要专用的服务系统而非沿用数字AI方案；Kairos通过使生成-执行循环成为系统核心，有效解决了物理AI任务独特的推理-执行交错问题，且在大规模集群下优势更明显。

Abstract: Physical AI is experiencing rapid growth with frontier foundation models increasing its capabilities across general environments. Physical AI tasks are characterized by inference properties that are markedly different from digital AI. They consist of multiple rounds of inference and action execution, generating a chunk of actions in each inference round, and asynchronously interleaving inference and execution. This makes existing digital AI serving systems unsuited for physical AI; a shortcoming that is critical for enabling their wide adoption, considering their size and the scale of the robot fleets they have to serve. To fill this gap, we design Kairos, the first multi-robot serving system that makes the generate-execute loop a first-class citizen, with active involvement in the execution phase. Across a wide range of physical AI models and robots, Kairos reduces the average end-to-end task latency by 31.8--66.5% over state-of-the-art digital AI serving practices, with gains scaling with the robot fleet size.

</details>


### [278] [Overcoming Dynamics-Blindness: Training-Free Pace-and-Path Correction for VLA Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11459)
*Yanyan Zhang,Chaoda Song,Vikash Singh,Xinpeng Li,Kai Ye,Zhe Hu,Zhongzhu Pu,Yu Yin,Vipin Chaudhary*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Pace-and-Path Correction，一种无需训练、闭式推理的操作符，用于包裹任何分块动作的VLA模型，通过正交分解为pace和path两个通道，在动态场景下显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型多基于单帧观测训练，对时序动态结构性地"盲视"，在非平稳场景下性能严重退化。现有方法要么需要昂贵重训练，要么存在延迟瓶颈和动作块间时序不一致问题。

Method: 提出Pace-and-Path Correction，一种训练无关的闭式推理操作符。从单一二次代价函数出发，联合最小化得到正交分解的两个通道：pace通道沿计划方向压缩执行，path通道施加正交空间偏移，共同吸收块窗口内感知到的动态。

Result: 在MoveBench诊断基准上，比最先进的训练无关包装器和动态自适应方法一致更优，在纯动态环境中绝对成功率提升高达28.8%，在动静混合环境中提升25.9%。

Conclusion: 提出的闭式推理操作符能有效弥补VLA模型对时序动态的盲视问题，无需额外训练，轻量高效，显著提升动态场景下的鲁棒性和成功率。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models achieve remarkable flexibility and generalization beyond classical control paradigms. However, most prevailing VLAs are trained under a single-frame observation paradigm, which leaves them structurally blind to temporal dynamics. Consequently, these models degrade severely in non-stationary scenarios, even when trained or finetuned on dynamic datasets. Existing approaches either require expensive retraining or suffer from latency bottlenecks and poor temporal consistency across action chunks. We propose Pace-and-Path Correction, a training-free, closed-form inference-time operator that wraps any chunked-action VLA. From a single quadratic cost, joint minimization yields a unified solution that decomposes orthogonally into two distinct channels. The pace channel compresses execution along the planned direction, while the path channel applies an orthogonal spatial offset, jointly absorbing the perceived dynamics within the chunk window. We evaluate our approach on a comprehensive diagnostic benchmark MoveBench designed to isolate motion as the sole controlled variable. Empirical results demonstrate that our framework consistently outperforms state-of-the-art training-free wrappers and dynamic-adaptive methods and improves success rates by up to 28.8% and 25.9% in absolute terms over foundational VLA models in dynamic-only and static-dynamic mixed environments, respectively.

</details>


### [279] [Offline Policy Evaluation for Manipulation Policies via Discounted Liveness Formulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11479)
*Hao Wang,Joshua Bowden,Colton Crosby,Somil Bansal*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于活性的Bellman算子框架，用于从稀疏奖励中进行离线策略评估，该框架通过将策略评估视为任务完成问题，得到对有限时间截断鲁棒的保守不动点值函数，实验证明该方法能更准确反映任务进展并显著减少截断偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人操控策略评估中，奖励稀疏、评估轨迹任务进展非单调且长度有限，导致标准方法（依赖Bellman方程/最优性原理）存在截断偏差，无法准确评估策略性能。

Method: 提出基于活性的Bellman算子（liveness-based Bellman operator），将策略评估建模为任务完成问题，推导出对有限时间截断鲁棒的保守不动点值函数，并给出收缩保证等理论性质。

Result: 在两项模拟操控任务（使用VLA模型和扩散策略）及一项布料折叠任务（使用人类演示）上，该方法相比TD(0)和蒙特卡洛策略评估等基线，更准确反映任务进展，显著降低截断偏差。

Conclusion: 所提框架有效解决了有限时间截断导致的标准策略评估方法失效问题，为稀疏奖励下的机器人策略评估提供了理论扎实且实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Policy evaluation is a fundamental component of the development and deployment pipeline for robotic policies. In modern manipulation systems, this problem is particularly challenging: rewards are often sparse, task progression of evaluation rollouts are often non-monotonic as the policies exhibit recovery behaviors, and evaluation rollouts are necessarily of finite length. This finite length introduces truncation bias, breaking the infinite-horizon assumptions underlying standard methods relying on Bellman equations/principle of optimality. In this work, we propose a framework for offline policy evaluation from sparse rewards based on a liveness-based Bellman operator. Our formulation interprets policy evaluation as a task-completion problem and yields a conservative fixed-point value function that is robust to finite-horizon truncation. We analyze the theoretical properties of the proposed operator, including contraction guarantees, and show how it encodes task progression while mitigating truncation bias. We evaluate our method on two simulated manipulation tasks using both a Vision-Language-Action model and a diffusion policy, and a cloth folding task using human demonstrations. Empirical results demonstrate that our approach more accurately reflects task progress and substantially reduces truncation bias, outperforming classical baselines such as TD(0) and Monte Carlo policy evaluation.

</details>


### [280] [Coordinated Diffusion: Generating Multi-Agent Behavior Without Multi-Agent Demonstrations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11485)
*Lasse Peters,Laura Ferranti,Javier Alonso-Mora,Andrea Bajcsy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出Coordinated Diffusion (CoDi)，一种利用单智能体演示数据学习多智能体协调策略的框架，无需协调演示，通过扩散模型和代价函数引导实现多智能体行为合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体模仿学习面临数据瓶颈：联合状态-动作空间随智能体数量指数增长，收集协调的多智能体演示成本极高。因此探索如何利用更易获取的单智能体数据来学习多智能体策略。

Method: CoDi框架将独立训练的单个智能体扩散策略通过用户定义的多智能体代价函数耦合起来。推导了一种新的扩散采样方案，其中扩散得分函数分解为预训练的单智能体基础策略加上代价驱动的引导项，该引导项可以无梯度方式估计，适用于黑盒、不可微的代价函数。

Result: 在仿真和双机械臂操作实验中，CoDi能够从单智能体数据中发现鲁棒的协调行为，比多智能体基线方法更数据高效，并展示了联合引导、基础策略支持和代价设计的重要性。

Conclusion: 单智能体演示数据需覆盖目标多智能体行为的支撑集，代价函数需从单智能体策略的乘积中促进期望行为。两者互补，共同实现有效的多智能体协调。

Abstract: Imitation learning powered by generative models has proven effective for modeling complex single-agent behaviors. However, teaching multi-agent systems, like multiple arms or vehicles, to coordinate through imitation learning is hindered by a fundamental data bottleneck: as the joint state-action space grows exponentially with the number of agents, collecting a sufficient amount of coordinated multi-agent demonstrations becomes extremely costly. In this work, we ask: how can we leverage single-agent demonstration data to learn multi-agent policies? We present Coordinated Diffusion (CoDi), a framework that couples independently trained single-agent diffusion policies through a user-defined multi-agent cost function, without requiring any coordinated demonstrations. We derive a new diffusion-based sampling scheme wherein the diffusion score function decomposes into independent, single-agent pre-trained base policies plus a cost-driven guidance term that coordinates these base policies into cohesive multi-agent behavior. We show that this guidance term can be estimated in a gradient-free manner, making CoDi applicable to black-box, non-differentiable cost functions without additional training. Theoretically and empirically, we analyze the conditions under which this composition can faithfully approximate a target multi-agent behavior. We find a complementary role for demonstration data versus the cost function: single-agent demonstrations must cover the support of the desired multi-agent behavior, while the cost function must promote desired behavior from this product of single-agent policies. Our results in simulation and hardware experiments of a two-arm manipulation task show that CoDi discovers robust coordinated behavior from single-agent data, is more data-efficient than multi-agent baselines, and highlights the importance of joint guidance, base policy support, and cost design.

</details>


### [281] [PRISM: : Planning and Reasoning with Intent in Simulated Embodied Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11534)
*Yunn Kang Lim,Pengzhan Sun,Ziyi Bai,Xun Xu,Angela Yao,Xulei Yang,Shijie Li*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: PRISM是一个诊断性基准，通过将任务失败归因于三个能力层级（基础能力、推理能力、长程能力）来评估LLM具身智能体，实验发现隐式意图解析是主要瓶颈，轻量级模型在长程任务中表现崩溃（成功率低至20%）且伴随过度推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅报告单一成功率指标，无法区分具身智能体失败的具体认知模块（如物体识别错误、子目标遗忘、动作序列不佳），因此需要一种能诊断失败原因的诊断性基准。

Method: 构建基于5个照片级真实多房间公寓（每套4-8间）的300个人工验证任务，分为三个能力层级；提供智能体无关的可执行动作API，支持LLM、VLM、符号规划器、RL策略和混合系统；可选配感知、记忆、规划探针进行组件级分析。

Result: 在7种当代LLM上的实验表明：在完美感知条件下空间接地不是主要失败来源；隐式意图解析是所有模型家族的显著瓶颈；长程协调任务存在能力悬崖——轻量级模型成功率低至20%，同时消耗比前沿模型更多的token，表现为补偿性过度推理而非真正规划能力。

Conclusion: PRISM提供了细粒度诊断能力，揭示了不同认知模块对不同模型的影响差异，尤其指出轻量级模型在长程任务中表现出的"假性推理"现象，为未来具身智能体系统改进提供了明确方向。

Abstract: When an LLM-based embodied agent fails at a household task, the culprit could be misidentified objects, forgotten sub-goals, or poor action sequencing -- yet existing benchmarks report only a single success rate, making it impossible to tell which cognitive module is responsible. We present PRISM, a diagnostic benchmark that reframes this problem: rather than asking only \textit{did the agent succeed?}, PRISM asks \textit{which capability is most likely responsible for failure?} Built on five photorealistic multi-room apartments (4--8 rooms each), PRISM structures 300 human-verified tasks into three capability tiers -- \textit{Basic Ability}, \textit{Reasoning Ability}, and \textit{Long-horizon Ability} -- that isolate perception-to-action grounding, implicit intent resolution, and sustained multi-step coordination respectively. PRISM exposes an agent-agnostic executable action API that allows arbitrary agents: LLM agents, VLM agents, symbolic planners, RL policies, and hybrid systems, to be evaluated end-to-end under the same benchmark protocol. To support deeper diagnosis, optional probes for perception, memory, and planning can be adopted, replaced, or bypassed entirely, enabling controlled component-level analysis when desired. Experiments on seven contemporary LLMs establish a clear hierarchy: explicit spatial grounding is not the dominant failure source under oracle perception, implicit intent resolution is a significant bottleneck for all model families, and long-horizon coordination exposes a stark capability cliff -- lightweight models collapse to as low as 20.0\% success while simultaneously consuming more tokens than their frontier counterparts, a signature of compensatory over-reasoning rather than genuine planning capability. Project page: \href{https://sj-li.com/PROJ/PRISM}{link}.

</details>


### [282] [RIO: Flexible Real-Time Robot I/O for Cross-Embodiment Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11564)
*Pablo Ortega-Kral,Eliot Xing,Arthur Bucker,Vernon Luk,Junseo Kim,Owen Kwon,Angchen Xie,Nikhil Sobanbabu,Yifu Yuan,Megan Lee,Deepam Ameria,Bhaswanth Ayapilla,Jaycie Bussell,Guanya Shi,Jonathan Francis,Jean Oh*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: RIO是一个开源Python框架，提供灵活轻量级的组件用于机器人控制、遥操作、数据格式化、传感器配置和策略部署，支持多种硬件平台和形态，验证了在三种机器人形态和四种硬件平台上部署VLA的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前通用跨形态机器人能力仍然难以实现，主要受限于碎片化的基础设施——大多数机器人代码高度特定于用户的具体设置，导致重用、回收或共享制品时产生大量开销。

Method: 设计并实现RIO（Robot I/O），一个开源Python框架，提供灵活的抽象层，使用户可以自由选择并切换不同组件（控制、遥操作、数据格式、传感器配置、策略部署），且只需最小的重新配置工作量。

Result: 在三种机器人形态（单臂、双臂、人形）和四种不同夹爪与相机的硬件平台上验证了VLA部署工作流；利用RIO收集的遥操作数据，微调了π0.5和GR00T等先进VLA模型，在拾放、折叠、刷碗等家务任务上取得效果。

Conclusion: 通过开源RIO框架，旨在加速社区在真实机器人硬件上进行机器人学习的研究进程。

Abstract: Despite recent efforts to collect multi-task, multi-embodiment datasets, to design recipes for training Vision-Language-Action models (VLAs), and to showcase these models on different robot platforms, generalist cross-embodiment robot capabilities remains a largely elusive ideal. Progress is limited by fragmented infrastructure: most robot code is highly specific to the exact setup the user decided on, which adds major overhead when attempting to reuse, recycle, or share artifacts between users. We present RIO (Robot I/O), an open source Python framework that provides flexible, lightweight components for robot control, teleoperation, data formatting, sensor configuration, and policy deployment across diverse hardware platforms and morphologies. RIO provides abstractions that enable users to make any choice and to switch between them, with minimal reconfiguration effort. We validate RIO on VLA deployment workflows across three morphologies (single-arm, bimanual, humanoid) and four hardware platforms with varying grippers and cameras. Using teleoperated data collected with RIO, we fine-tune state-of-the-art VLAs including $π_{0.5}$ and GR00T on household tasks such as pick-and-place, folding, and bowl scrubbing. By open sourcing all our efforts, we hope the community can accelerate their pace of robot learning on real-world robot hardware. Additional details at: https://robot-i-o.github.io

</details>


### [283] [Sampling-Based Follow-the-Leader Motion Planning for Manipulator-Mounted Continuum Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11618)
*Chengnan Shentu,Nicholas Baldassini,Oluwagbotemi D. Iseoluwa,Radian Gondokaryono,Jessica Burgner-Kahrs*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本论文提出了一种用于机械臂搭载的连续体机器人（CR）的"跟随领导者"（FTL）运动规划方法。核心思路是将全局形状搜索与基座姿态解耦，通过闭式几何构造计算基座姿态，避免在线优化。实验在120条模拟路径上实现0%尖端误差和1.9%平均形状偏差，成功率100%，并在6自由度肌腱驱动CR上进行了实物验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有FTL方法通常假设固定基座或单自由度插入机构，无法适用于将CR安装在具有全驱动SE(3)基座姿态的机械臂上的实际系统。作者旨在解决这一局限性，让机械臂搭载的CR也能实现高效的FTL运动。

Method: 提出基于采样的运动规划器，将全局形状搜索与基座姿态确定解耦。基座姿态通过闭式几何构造计算，避免在线迭代优化。支持通用前向模型，将大部分计算转移至离线完成。

Result: 在3个测试类别的120条模拟路径上，实现了0%尖端误差、1.9%平均形状偏差（相对于机器人长度），成功率100%。在6自由度肌腱驱动CR+串联机械臂平台上验证了实用性。

Conclusion: 该方法提供了分辨率完备的形状搜索和收敛的尖端跟踪理论保证，能够高效、准确地实现机械臂搭载CR的FTL运动规划，具备良好的实际应用前景。

Abstract: Follow-the-leader (FTL) motion exploits the unique morphology of continuum robots (CRs) to navigate confined spaces by having the body retrace the path of the tip. While extensively studied, existing FTL methods typically assume a fixed base or a single degree-of-freedom insertion mechanism, limiting their applicability to practical systems in which CRs are mounted on robotic manipulators with fully actuated SE(3) base pose. This paper presents a sampling-based motion planner for FTL motion of manipulator-mounted CRs that jointly considers robot configuration and base pose. The key idea is to decouple global shape search from base pose determination by computing the base pose through a closed-form geometric construction, thereby avoiding iterative optimization during online planning. The approach supports general forward models and enables efficient planning by shifting the majority of computation offline. We establish theoretical guarantees including resolution complete shape search and converging tip tracking throughout waypoint traversal and interpolation. Experiments on 120 simulated paths over 3 test classes demonstrate 0% tip error and 1.9% mean shape deviation (w.r.t. robot length) at 100% success rate. We validate the practicality of our approach on a 6-DOF tendon-driven CR mounted on a serial manipulator. Code and visualization available at https://continuumroboticslab.github.io/sb-ftl-cr-planner/.

</details>


### [284] [Nautilus: From One Prompt to Plug-and-Play Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11665)
*Yufeng Jin,Jianfei Guo,Xiaogang Jia,Yu Deng,Zechu Li,Han Liu,Weiran Liao,Vignesh Prasad,Mathias Franzius,Gerhard Neumann,Georgia Chalvatzaki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NAUTILUS 是一个开源工具，能够将单一用户指令（如"用基准B评估策略A"）自动转化为可复现的评估、微调和部署工作流，通过即插即用的智能体技能集、类型化契约和自动化验证，大幅降低机器人学习研究中跨策略族、跨基准的工程负担。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习研究在不同策略族、基准测试和真实机器人之间高度碎片化，各实现相互纠缠，工程上难以移植和复用。通用编码智能体缺乏机器人研究所需的先验知识和验证实践，无法规模化弥合这一鸿沟。

Method: NAUTILUS 提供：(1) 即插即用的智能体技能集，包含机器人研究的蒸馏先验；(2) 策略、模拟器/基准和真实机器人之间的类型化契约；(3) 统一接口和执行环境；(4) 可信任的智能体编码工作流，每个里程碑都有自动验证和测试。它能自动生成现有实现的适配器和容器，也能封装新接入的组件，通过"舱室化"扩展。

Result: NAUTILUS 能够自动将用户指令转化为端到端工作流，自动生成跨组件的适配器，实现无需手工胶水代码的交叉验证覆盖扩展，以可扩展的研究平台而非手工策划框架的方式运行。

Conclusion: NAUTILUS 旨在像鹦鹉螺壳通过增加舱室生长一样，通过舱室化单元扩展执行能力，成为机器人学习生态系统中规模化跨族复现和评估的研究工具，有效降低工程负担。

Abstract: Robot learning research is fragmented across policy families, benchmark suites, and real robots; each implementation is entangled with the others in a complex combination matrix, making it an engineering nightmare to port any single element. General-purpose coding agents may occasionally bridge specific setups, but cannot close this gap at scale because they lack the procedural priors and validation practices that characterize robotics research workflows. We propose NAUTILUS, an open-source harness that turns a single user prompt -- for example, "Evaluate policy A with benchmark B" -- into ready-to-use reproduction, evaluation, fine-tuning, and deployment workflows. NAUTILUS provides: plug-and-play agent skill sets with distilled priors from robotics research; typed contracts among policies, simulators/benchmarks, and real-world robots; unified interfaces and execution environments; and a trustworthy agentic coding workflow with explicit, automated validation, and testing at each milestone. NAUTILUS can not only automatically generate the required adapters and containers for existing implementations, but also wrap and onboard new or user-provided policies, simulators/benchmarks, and robots, all connected via a uniform interface. This expands cross-validation coverage without hand-written glue code. Like a nautilus shell that grows by adding chambers, NAUTILUS scales by extending its execution in chambered units, making it a research harness for scalability rather than a hand-curated framework, and aiming to reduce the engineering burden of cross-family reproduction and evaluation in the ever-growing robot learning ecosystem.

</details>


### [285] [A Proprioceptive-Only Benchmark for Quadruped State Estimation: ATE, RPE, and Runtime Trade-offs Between Filters and Smoothers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.11674)
*Ylenia Nisticò,João Carlos Virgolino Soares,Joan Solà,Claudio Semini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文在四足机器人大数据集上对比了三种本体状态估计器（MUSE、IEKF、IS），发现短时精度相近，IEKF和IS长时精度更优，并公开了全部评测代码以支持可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为四足机器人领域从业者提供关于三种主流状态估计器在精度与计算时间上的清晰对比指导，帮助根据应用约束选择合适的方案。

Method: 在固定硬件/软件栈上，使用GrandTour Dataset的CYN-1序列，以ATE评估长时精度、RPE评估短时精度，并测量每次更新的计算时间。

Result: 三种方法的RPE（短时精度）整体相似；IEKF和IS的ATE（长时精度）低于MUSE；运行时间结果揭示了精度与延迟之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 本研究为读者选择适合其应用约束的状态估计器提供了简洁的精度与成本快照，并通过开源评测代码保证了完全可复现性。

Abstract: We compare three state-of-the-art proprioceptive state estimators for quadruped robots: MUSE [1], the Invariant Extended Kalman Filter (IEKF) [2], and the Invariant Smoother (IS) [3], on the CYN-1 sequence of the GrandTour Dataset [4]. Our goal is to give practitioners clear guidance on accuracy and computation time: we report long-term accuracy (Absolute Trajectory Error, ATE), short-term accuracy (translational and rotational Relative Pose Error, RPE), and per-update computation time on a fixed hardware/software stack. On this dataset, RPEs are broadly similar across methods, while IEKF and IS achieve a lower ATE than MUSE. Runtime results highlight the accuracy-latency trade-offs across the three approaches. In the discussion, we outline the evaluation choices used to ensure a fair comparison and analyze factors that influence short-horizon metrics. Overall, this study provides a concise snapshot of accuracy and cost, helping readers choose an estimator that fits their application constraints, with all evaluation code and documentation released open-source at https://github.com/iit-DLSLab/state_estimation_benchmark for full reproducibility.

</details>
